R270 is the 16-pair intersection of R264 and R268. It has seven training hits,
Sorafenib-thyroid cancer as its DRH hit, and eight partition-safe predictions:

- Sorafenib for papillary thyroid carcinoma and Plexosarcoma.
- Midostaurin, Axitinib, and Ponatinib for Plexosarcoma.
- Regorafenib for thyroid cancer and papillary thyroid carcinoma.
- Repaglinide for neonatal diabetes.

R270 enters the treatment-free visible Pareto surface. R264 enters the
treatment-free validation surface. R268 enters the treatment-free visible
surface. R265 enters global validation and both treatment-free surfaces.

R266 has similar top-three coverage but worse validation than R264/R268 and is
rejected. R267 removes Eslicarbazepine because duplicate extractions collapse
to one publication and loses all DRH support; it is rejected. R269's sole pair
is Sunitinib-Plexosarcoma in training. Its 100% visible precision has no novel
prediction and no validation support, so it is a source-coverage diagnostic,
not a useful rule, and is rejected.

One attempted raw identity query for the one-pair SIGNOR holdout was not run
because it would have bypassed partition filtering and risked exposing a
TTD-only pair. The identity was obtained only after the blind-safe comparator
classified it as training.

After this batch the raw library surfaces are 32 global visible, 9 global
validation, 18 treatment-free visible, and 9 treatment-free validation. These
raw counts include empirical entries later rejected on mechanism or utility
grounds; the deadline audit will report accepted and raw surfaces separately.

## Hypothesis Batch 33: Combination, Formulation, and Metabolite Coverage

Hypothesis: a combination drug whose components cover complementary disease
targets could create a nonlinear treatment rule.

This branch was blocked by graph coverage and existing-family duplication:

- Active-metabolite target propagation and prodrug/active-moiety transfer are
  already represented by R132-R134 and R138-R140.
- Product formulation replacement was tested in R242.
- Drug `HAS_PART` coverage is only 67 edges from 37 drugs to 22 generic
  ChemicalEntity nodes, plus 19 edges involving 15 Drug ontology-class nodes.
  All inspected composition edges are SemMedDB-only.
- A broad unbounded inventory over every Drug-to-Chemical predicate ran for
  more than 60 seconds and was terminated rather than consuming Neo4j
  resources after the targeted `HAS_PART` inventory had already shown the
  relevant limitation.

The graph needs normalized active-ingredient, fixed-dose-combination,
stoichiometry, and parent/metabolite exposure relations before a component-wise
module-coverage rule can be interpreted mechanistically. No new rule was
cataloged.

## Hypothesis Batch 34: Phenotype Triangulation and Model Orthologs

Hypothesis: a causal target should explain disease manifestations, and a mouse
ortholog phenotype can provide an independent in vivo bridge when direct human
gene-to-phenotype evidence is sparse.

Direct gene-phenotype triangulation failed at the graph layer:

- 2,706 Gene-to-Phenotype edges cover 2,141 genes but only 65 phenotype nodes.
- MGI contributes 1,568 EFO and 1,067 HP edges; RGD contributes 34; direct HPO
  annotations contribute only 37 edges for five human genes.
- Disease phenotypes contain 171,469 HPO edges over 9,473 phenotype nodes.
- No aggregated gene-disease pair has an exact shared phenotype or a shared
  HPO ancestor within two subclass steps.

The model annotations are unusually coarse: increased heart weight annotates
491 genes, small kidney 327, decreased anxiety response 289, insulin
resistance 252, and cardiomegaly 208. The graph needs complete gene-to-HPO or
MP-to-HPO mappings with genotype and zygosity before direct phenotype-module
coverage can be used.

PANTHER orthology is much richer: 75,126 `ORTHOLOGOUS_TO` edges. Human-to-mouse
paths reach 2,582 MGI phenotype assertions over 2,045 human genes, 1,965 mouse
genes, and 29 phenotype nodes. Exact phenotype concordance supports seven
aggregated human gene-disease pairs; a two-step ontology bridge expands this to
101 pairs but was not used because the direct phenotype vocabulary is already
too coarse.

The first four taxon-aware aggregate queries failed because a diagnostic query
coalesced `in_taxon` with scalar fallback properties and then called
`toString()` on string arrays:

```text
Invalid input for function 'toString()': Expected a String, Float, Integer,
Boolean, Temporal or Duration, got: StringArray[NCBITaxon:9606]
```

`apoc.convert.toList` is unavailable. A type inventory showed all 72,412
non-null `in_taxon` properties are string lists, so the executable correction
is explicit membership:

```cypher
'NCBITaxon:9606' IN coalesce(human_gene.in_taxon, [])
'NCBITaxon:10090' IN coalesce(mouse_gene.in_taxon, [])
```

The exact model-supported family is:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (human_gene:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(human_gene)
MATCH (human_gene)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (human_gene)-[:ORTHOLOGOUS_TO]-(mouse_gene:Gene)
      -[model_evidence:HAS_PHENOTYPE]->(phenotype:PhenotypicFeature)
      <-[:HAS_PHENOTYPE]-(object)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND 'NCBITaxon:9606' IN coalesce(human_gene.in_taxon, [])
  AND 'NCBITaxon:10090' IN coalesce(mouse_gene.in_taxon, [])
  AND 'infores:mgi' IN
      coalesce(model_evidence.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifacts:

- `results/runs/20260812T145808Z/summary.json`
- `results/runs/20260812T150035Z/summary.json`

| Rule | Directional interpretation | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R271 | GOF decrease | 17 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R272 | LOF increase | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R273 | GOF increase control | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R274 | LOF decrease control | 7 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R275 | GOF decrease, no opposite assertion | 13 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

All three LOF-restoration predictions also occur in the LOF-worsening set:
Imatinib, human insulin, and Crizotinib for Donohue syndrome. Four GOF pairs
also have both directions: Diltiazem, Testosterone, and Bepridil for Timothy
syndrome, and Diazoxide for Cantu syndrome.

Representative evidence:

- Diltiazem decreases CACNA1C with 43 evidence items but also increases it with
  two; the CACNA1C mouse ortholog and Timothy syndrome share cardiomegaly.
- Diazoxide both increases and decreases KCNJ8 and ABCC9; mouse models and Cantu
  syndrome again share cardiomegaly.
- Insulin has both directions on INSR for Donohue syndrome and shared insulin
  resistance.
- Tolbutamide has five inhibitory publications for ABCC9, no opposite
  assertion, and exact cardiomegaly concordance through the mouse ortholog.

R271-R274 are rejected as general rules because they are uncalibrated and
direction-conflicted. R275 is retained only as a high-mechanism exploratory
candidate: 13 partition-safe predictions across Timothy and Cantu syndromes,
with no training or DRH support. It is not a Pareto survivor.

## Hypothesis Batch 35: Molecular-Activity Correction

Hypothesis: Gene `ENABLES` MolecularActivity is sufficiently direct to compose
with GOF/LOF disease direction, unlike generic process membership.

Coverage:

- 224,732 GOA `ENABLES` edges connect 61,401 genes to 4,951 activities.
- CTD supplies 1,201 qualified Drug-to-MolecularActivity decreases and 789
  increases.
- Gene-enable degree is minimum 1, p25 3, median 7, p75 20, p90 58, maximum
  21,109.

The reproducible feature builder and Neo4j graph now materialize:

```cypher
MATCH (gene:Gene)-[:ENABLES]->(activity:MolecularActivity)
WITH activity, count(DISTINCT gene) AS degree
SET activity._miner_gene_enable_degree = degree
```

All 4,951 activities were updated. The family uses the predeclared p90 bound
of 58 without trying additional cutoffs:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(activity:MolecularActivity)
      <-[:ENABLES]-(driver:Gene)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:ctd' IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND activity._miner_gene_enable_degree <= 58
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T150744Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Directional interpretation | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R276 | GOF activity decrease | 39 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R277 | LOF activity increase | 149 | 0 | 0 | 1 |
| R278 | GOF activity increase control | 17 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R279 | LOF activity decrease control | 306 | 1 | 0 | 0 |

The only visible hit is the worsening-control Ripretinib-Plexosarcoma path.
Fourteen LOF corrective pairs also occur in the worsening set. Example paths
show why:

- ATP both increases and decreases voltage-gated chloride channel activity for
  CLCNKB-related Bartter disease.
- Pexidartinib decreases succinate dehydrogenase activity connected to SDHB and
  SDHD for pheochromocytoma, but the Gene2Phenotype assertions are marked GOF,
  inconsistent with the expected tumor-suppressor mechanism.
- Dinoprostone decreases ATP1A2-enabled sodium/potassium transporter activity
  for developmental epileptic encephalopathy 98.
- Tolbutamide decreases KCNJ8-enabled ATP-activated inward-rectifier potassium
  channel activity for Cantu syndrome.

CTD activity effects are context-dependent, and a gene can enable an activity
without the disease variant increasing the activity as a whole. R276-R279 are
rejected as general rules.

### Cross-Family Consensus

The intersection of contradiction-gated exact mouse support (R275) and compact
GOF activity correction (R276) contains exactly one partition-safe prediction:
Tolbutamide-Cantu syndrome.

R280 strengthens the intersection by requiring distinct GOF genes in the two
branches. Tolbutamide inhibits an exact mouse-phenotype-supported Cantu driver
and independently decreases the compact potassium-channel activity enabled by
a second Cantu GOF driver. The executable query is stored directly in
`rules/campaign-i09.toml`.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T151110Z/summary.json`.

R280 has one candidate, no training, DRH, contraindication, or target edge. It
is retained as a single high-mechanism exploratory prediction, not as a
calibrated or Pareto rule.

## Batch 29 Correction: Cellular-Component Degree Property

The earlier conclusion that R253/R254 had zero measured-degree coverage was
caused by a second property-name bug. The feature builder stores cellular
component membership as:

```text
_miner_component_gene_member_degree
```

but the family compiler queried:

```text
_miner_gene_member_degree
```

The initial `coalesce(..., 0)` run was invalid because it admitted missing
values, but the first fail-fast correction still used the wrong property. The
compiler and tests now require:

```cypher
context._miner_component_gene_member_degree <= 54
```

Corrected run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T151307Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R253 | 6 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 16.67% |
| R254 | 6 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 16.67% |

All six pairs concern neonatal diabetes mellitus. The training hit is
Glyburide; the five partition-safe predictions are Tolbutamide,
Chlorpropamide, Glipizide, Repaglinide, and Gliclazide. Every path covers
KCNJ11 at disease rank 1 and ABCC8 at rank 2 in the GOA inward-rectifying
potassium-channel component, whose measured gene-member degree is 12.

R253 remains a high-mechanism exploratory candidate despite not reaching a
Pareto surface. R254 is rejected as redundant because the direct adverse gate
removes no pair. This append-only correction supersedes the zero-coverage
interpretation in Batch 29; the original failed runs remain documented.

## Batch 36: Feature-Property Implementation Audit

The cellular-component property bug motivated a static and live audit of every
`_miner_*` feature referenced by `campaign-i09.toml` and
`rule_families.py`.

Static command:

```bash
rg -o --no-filename '_miner_[A-Za-z0-9_]+' \
  rules/campaign-i09.toml src/miner2049er/rule_families.py | sort -u
```

The resulting set was compared with the properties built in
`src/miner2049er/rule_features.py`. Every referenced property has a builder.
Manual class/property checks confirmed the intended pairings:

- `BiologicalProcess._miner_gene_member_degree`
- `CellularComponent._miner_component_gene_member_degree`
- `CellularComponent._miner_component_active_gene_degree`
- `GeneFamily._miner_gene_family_member_degree`
- `Cell._miner_human_gene_expression_degree`
- `MolecularActivity._miner_gene_enable_degree`

Live semantic audit:

```cypher
MATCH (n)
WITH labels(n) AS labels, n
WHERE n._miner_gene_enable_degree IS NOT NULL
RETURN labels, count(*) AS nodes,
       min(n._miner_gene_enable_degree) AS min_degree,
       max(n._miner_gene_enable_degree) AS max_degree
ORDER BY nodes DESC
```

All 4,951 nodes carrying `_miner_gene_enable_degree` are
`MolecularActivity` nodes; the range is 1 to 21,109.

The audit found a rebuild-safety defect rather than another scoring defect:
`_miner_gene_enable_degree` was built but omitted from both `CLEAR_QUERIES` and
`INDEX_QUERIES`. A subsequent rebuild after graph changes could therefore
retain stale values on activities no longer reached by `ENABLES`, and the
molecular-activity rules lacked a supporting range index. The clear clause,
index, and regression test have been added. Targeted verification is 6 tests
passing and Ruff clean.

No rule metrics changed. The graph limitation is procedural: derived feature
properties are not schema-declared, so class/property mismatches are detectable
only through tests and live audits unless a feature registry is added.

## Batch 37: Signed Gene-Regulatory Cascades

### Hypothesis and inventory

A drug may act through a different gene than the disease driver: perturbing a
bound regulator could propagate through a signed gene-to-gene edge and correct
an explicit GOF or LOF driver. Aggregate gene-to-gene inventory found:

| Relationship | Edges |
|---|---:|
| `PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH` | 518,082 |
| `AFFECTS` | 334,595 |
| `REGULATES` | 21,107 |
| `DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH` | 16,194 |

Of the signed gene-to-gene `AFFECTS` edges, 332,838 are from
`infores:text-mining-provider-targeted` and 1,757 from SemMedDB. Although all
336,453 causal gene-to-gene `AFFECTS` edges have a publication and 126,710 have
at least two publication entries, this channel produced tens of thousands of
candidate pairs per sign combination and was rejected before cataloging as too
text-mining-dominated.

SIGNOR provides a cleaner channel: 12,427 `REGULATES` edges marked
`upregulated` and 6,958 marked `downregulated`. Most still have only one
publication.

### Executable family

Rules R281-R284 in `rules/campaign-i09.toml` implement the following pattern:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (regulator:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[drug_effect:AFFECTS]->(regulator)
MATCH (regulator)-[gene_effect:REGULATES]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (driver)-[evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
WHERE regulator <> driver
  AND drug_effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND drug_effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND gene_effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND 'infores:signor' IN
      coalesce(gene_effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
```

R281 composes GOF-corrective signs, R282 is its worsening control, R283
composes LOF-corrective signs, and R284 is its worsening control.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T152208Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Interpretation | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R281 | GOF corrective | 130 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R282 | GOF worsening | 85 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R283 | LOF corrective | 290 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R284 | LOF worsening | 327 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

Comparator ablation found 45 GOF pairs and 77 LOF pairs in both the corrective
and worsening sets. Across all eligible paths, 46 GOF and 94 LOF pairs have at
least two distinct sign combinations.

Partition-safe examples explain the conflict:

- Yohimbine, Landiolol, and Sulfisoxazole reach GOF GNAS in
  McCune-Albright syndrome through different GPCR-to-GNAS SIGNOR assertions.
- Erlotinib and Dacomitinib reach LOF ABCA1 in Tangier disease through
  EGFR-downregulates-ABCA1-stability assertions.
- Erlotinib reaches LOF UBE3A in Angelman syndrome through an
  ABL1-downregulates-UBE3A-activity assertion.

The regulatory assertions are biologically contextual, but the graph does not
encode the cell type, stimulus, or experimental state needed to compose their
signs safely. Excluding contradictory pairs would still leave no visible
calibration hits and would select on missing context rather than restore it.
R281-R284 are therefore rejected, and no threshold variant was attempted.

## Batch 38: Ontology-Aware Phenotype Support and Disease Reversal

### HP phenotype neighborhood

The MGI gene/model phenotype branch contains 1,568 EFO and 1,067 HP edges,
whereas all 171,469 disease phenotype edges use HP. Ontology expansion was
therefore restricted to HP.

The HP descendant-degree distribution for 4,982 nodes is minimum 1, median 3,
p75 11, p90 35, p95 86, and maximum 16,149. The executable one-parent probe
was:

```cypher
MATCH (mouse_gene:Gene)-[model:HAS_PHENOTYPE]->
      (mouse_phenotype:PhenotypicFeature)
      -[:SUBCLASS_OF]->(ancestor:PhenotypicFeature)
      <-[:SUBCLASS_OF]-(disease_phenotype:PhenotypicFeature)
      <-[:HAS_PHENOTYPE]-(object:Disease)
WHERE 'infores:mgi' IN coalesce(model.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND mouse_phenotype.id STARTS WITH 'HP:'
  AND disease_phenotype.id STARTS WITH 'HP:'
  AND ancestor._miner_phenotype_descendant_degree <= $degree
```

At p90 this produced 6,727 gene-disease pairs. The largest hubs were abnormal
blood gas level (4,797 pairs), hormone resistance (747), abnormal oral
physiology (550), and abnormality of salivation (318). At p75 it still produced
1,290 pairs through four ancestors. At the median cutoff it collapsed to only
the salivation ancestor, connecting two model genes to 159 diseases.

The phenotype ontology does not provide a useful specificity regime for this
bridge: tighter degree limits select idiosyncratic hubs rather than more exact
mechanisms. No rule was cataloged and no drug-level identities were queried.

### Disease-effect reversal blocker

A separate hypothesis proposed reversing disease-induced gene dysregulation
with an oppositely signed drug effect. The graph contains only two
disease-to-gene `AFFECTS` edges, both unqualified SemMedDB assertions. The
reverse gene-to-disease branch has 1,752 edges and disease-to-process has 4,771,
but all lack aspect and direction qualifiers. The required disease sign is
therefore absent, so this family was blocked before candidate generation.

## Batch 39: Nonlinear Physical Connector Modules

### Hypothesis and executable family

A drug may inhibit a non-causal network connector that physically contacts at
least two top-three causal genes for the same disease:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(target:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
MATCH (target)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]-(driver:Gene)
MATCH (driver)-[evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object:Disease)
WHERE target <> driver
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
WITH subject, target, object, collect(DISTINCT driver) AS drivers
WHERE size(drivers) >= 2
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

The direct gene-interaction degree distribution over 5,593 genes is median 2,
p75 5, p90 10, p95 17, p99 50, and maximum 281. All but two direct gene-gene
interaction edges are SIGNOR-derived. The unconstrained motif produced 171
pairs and 191 target modules; every connector was outside the derived disease
causal-gene set.

Rules R285-R288 are executable in `rules/campaign-i09.toml`. R286 is an
increased-target control, R287 adds the p90 connector-degree bound, and R288
requires the connector and both drivers to share one process with at most 100
gene members.

Run artifacts:

- `results/runs/20260812T153005Z/summary.json`
- `results/runs/20260812T153315Z/summary.json`

| Rule | Variant | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R285 | decreased connector | 171 | 4 | 1 | 3 |
| R286 | increased control | 35 | 1 | 0 | 1 |
| R287 | degree <= 10 | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R288 | shared compact process | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

R285's DRH hit is Sorafenib-thyroid cancer through MAPK1 and MAPK3 connectors
to BRAF and RET. It is also the sole validation intersection with the much
stronger R268 binding-provenance rule. MAPK1 and MAPK3 have direct-interaction
degrees 262 and 214. Donepezil-epilepsy is a contraindicated novel example
through degree-269 GSK3B to SCN8A and SCN2A. Erlotinib-Crohn disease is less
hub-dominated, through degree-15 RIPK2 to ATG16L1 and NOD2, but remains
unlabeled.

The family depends on interaction hubs, adds no independent validation hit,
and loses all labels under either degree or pathway specificity. R285-R288 are
rejected.

### Pareto recomputation after Batches 37-39

After synchronizing catalog rejection statuses, the accepted/status-filtered
surfaces contain 32 global visible, nine global validation, 17 treatment-free
visible, and nine treatment-free validation rules. None of R281-R288 enters a
surface.

## Batch 40: Phenotype-Level Therapeutic Decomposition

### Hypothesis and coverage

A drug may have two independent reasons to treat a disease: it treats one of
the disease's phenotypes and directly inhibits a top-ranked causal gene.
Exact drug-to-phenotype `TREATS` edges comprise 2,933 clinical-trial, 775
DrugCentral, 526 drug-approval, 262 DRH, and 227 TTD assertions. DRH and TTD
phenotype-treatment edges are excluded from rule evidence.

The phenotype disease-member-degree distribution over 9,473 phenotypes is
minimum 1, median 3, p75 10, p90 34, p95 70, and maximum 2,237. The executable
base family is:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[symptom_treatment:TREATS]->
      (phenotype:PhenotypicFeature)
      <-[:HAS_PHENOTYPE]-(object:Disease)
MATCH (subject)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(gene:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(gene)
MATCH (gene)-[evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
WHERE none(
    source IN coalesce(symptom_treatment.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
```

R290 adds phenotype disease degree at most 34. R291 excludes contraindication,
exacerbation, and generic cause edges. R292 excludes only contraindication and
exacerbation because the generic `CAUSES` branch proved noisy.

Run artifacts:

- `results/runs/20260812T153620Z/summary.json`
- `results/runs/20260812T153827Z/summary.json`
- `results/runs/20260812T154036Z/summary.json`

| Rule | Variant | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R289 | base | 22 | 5 | 0 | 2 | 22.73% |
| R290 | phenotype degree <= 34 | 13 | 4 | 0 | 2 | 30.77% |
| R291 | all adverse predicates gated | 10 | 3 | 0 | 0 | 30.00% |
| R292 | contraindication/exacerbation gated | 11 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 36.36% |

Known paths are coherent:

- Imatinib, Dasatinib, and Ponatinib treat Ph-positive acute lymphoblastic
  leukemia and inhibit top-ranked ABL1/BCR for BCR-ABL1-positive CML.
- Tafamidis (`Fx-1006A`) treats transthyretin cardiac amyloidosis and inhibits
  top-ranked TTR for familial amyloid neuropathy.

Partition-safe predictions include Tofacitinib-polycythemia vera through
thromboembolism/JAK2 and Tafamidis-wild-type ATTR amyloidosis through
transthyretin cardiac amyloidosis/TTR. The family also exposes symptomatic
counterexamples: Heparin-antithrombin deficiency and Propafenone-Brugada are
explicitly contraindicated, while Dapagliflozin-familial renal glucosuria may
phenocopy rather than correct SLC5A2 loss.

R291 is rejected because its generic `CAUSES` anti-join removes the known
Imatinib-CML treatment. R289 is retained as the higher-coverage family member
and R292 as the precision-skewed negative-evidence variant. R290 remains the
parameterized parent but is empirically dominated by R292. The family receives
a medium mechanism score because treating a disease manifestation can be
clinically useful without modifying the underlying disorder.

## Batch 41: Transporter-Supported Tissue Delivery

### Hypothesis and correction

A drug-substrate transporter expressed in the affected anatomy might support
tissue delivery for an independent causal target expressed in the same tissue.
The graph contains 535 transporter-activity-supported gene/drug substrate pairs
before taxon filtering; 835 substrate edges are ChEMBL, with one each from
WOMBAT-PK and DrugCentral.

R293 requires:

```cypher
MATCH (transporter:Gene)-[:HAS_SUBSTRATE]->(subject:Drug)
MATCH (transporter)-[:ENABLES]->(activity:MolecularActivity)
      -[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->
      (:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0005215'})
MATCH (subject)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(target:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
MATCH (target)-[evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (transporter)-[transport_expression:EXPRESSED_IN]->
      (anatomy:AnatomicalEntity)
      <-[target_expression:EXPRESSED_IN]-(target)
MATCH (object)-[:LOCATED_IN|OCCURS_IN]->(anatomy)
```

The first run returned 40 pairs, five training hits, and four adverse pairs,
but path inspection exposed mouse `Abcb1a` as the transporter for
Phenytoin-epilepsy. This result was invalid because the rule omitted taxon
constraints. The executable family was corrected to require human transporter
and target genes and rerun.

Corrected run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T154424Z/summary.json`.

R293 then returned 37 pairs, four training hits, no DRH hit, and four adverse
pairs (10.81% visible precision). Examples show the central ambiguity:

- Imatinib-CML uses ABCB1 efflux in granulocytes.
- Quercetin-Alzheimer disease uses ABCB1 efflux in hippocampal anatomy.
- D-glucose-hyperglycemia uses GLUT transporters in pancreatic islets and is
  explicitly adverse.
- Enalapril-angioedema uses SLC15A1 intestinal transport despite angioedema
  being a known ACE-inhibitor toxicity.

R294 excludes transporters enabling a descendant of
`GO:0015562` efflux transmembrane transporter activity. Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T154657Z/summary.json`. It returned 29 pairs, one
training hit, no DRH hit, and three adverse pairs (3.45% visible precision).

Substrate edges do not state uptake versus efflux, and even ontology-level
efflux exclusion does not establish inward delivery. R293-R294 are rejected.

## Batch 42: Direct Protein-Abundance Correction

### Hypothesis and controls

Direct variant correction had previously used only the broad
`activity_or_abundance` aspect. Explicit abundance edges support a narrower
protein-dosage hypothesis:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'loss_of_function_variant_form'
```

The graph has four LOF/increased pairs and two LOF/decreased controls. There is
no direct-binding GOF/decreased abundance pair, so a GOF degradation rule is
coverage-blocked.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T155004Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R295 | LOF abundance increased | 4 | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| R296 | LOF abundance decreased control | 2 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

R295's known treatments are strongly mechanistic:

- Migalastat increases GLA abundance for Fabry disease; the abundance edge has
  ChEMBL, EMA, and FDA documents and the binding has SIGNOR, ChEMBL, and
  BindingDB support.
- Miglustat increases GAA abundance for glycogen storage disease II, supported
  by PMID 36542959.

The two predictions are Estradiol and Enzalutamide for androgen insensitivity
through increased AR abundance. They are not convincing treatment mechanisms:
abundance does not restore receptor function, and Enzalutamide antagonizes AR.

Requiring the LOF driver to enable a descendant of catalytic activity retains
only the two known enzyme-chaperone treatments. Excluding any drug with a
decreased activity/abundance edge to the driver retains only one pair. Both
refinements have no prediction and are therefore not cataloged as usable rules.
R295 remains a high-mechanism exploratory rule with a clearly documented
functional-restoration limitation; R296 is the zero-hit matched control.

## Batch 43: Direct LOF Splicing Rescue

The graph contains 391 LOF driver/disease pairs connected to non-directional
CTD drug splicing effects, but only two survive direct drug-driver binding.
R297 implements:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'splicing'
  AND 'infores:ctd' IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'loss_of_function_variant_form'
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T155320Z/summary.json`.

Both candidates are training treatments: Eteplirsen and Viltolarsen for
Duchenne muscular dystrophy. There is no prediction and no contraindication.
The 100% visible precision is therefore not useful for discovery. R297 is
rejected for zero predictive output despite its high mechanistic coherence.
The graph gap remains explicit splice direction or corrected-transcript
product, which would permit broader rescue rules without relying on direct
binding to known exon-skipping drugs.

## Batch 44: Direct Activity Correction and Molecular-Function Classes

### Hypothesis

An explicit drug effect on the activity of a directly bound causal disease
gene should be more mechanistic than the earlier broad
`activity_or_abundance` rules. The causal direction supplies a matched
corrective and worsening control:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T155548Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Variant/effect direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R298 | GOF/decreased, corrective | 861 | 10 | 0 | 0 | 1.16% |
| R299 | LOF/increased, corrective | 254 | 2 | 0 | 1 | 0.79% |
| R300 | GOF/increased, worsening | 68 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0% |
| R301 | LOF/decreased, worsening | 941 | 2 | 0 | 1 | 0.21% |

An ablation excluding any drug-driver pair with an opposite-direction activity
edge barely changed the GOF rule (861 to 855 pairs) and reduced the LOF rule
only to 214 pairs. The problem is therefore not primarily explicit
directional contradiction. R298 contains plausible kinase treatments among its
known hits, but its predictions range from potentially coherent channelopathy
interventions to context-free text-mined activity changes. R299 recovers
Migalastat-Fabry disease and Sapropterin-phenylketonuria, but its predictions
are dominated by sedative/epilepsy associations without evidence that the
activity increase restores the relevant deficient function. Neither
corrective rule has a DRH validation hit. R298 and R299 are rejected as broad
families.

### Ontology-native activity classes

The next hypothesis used the graph's GO molecular-function hierarchy as a node
constraint rather than tuning a numerical threshold:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[:ENABLES]->(:MolecularActivity)
      -[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->(:MolecularActivity {id: $activity_root})
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T155906Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Function class and direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R302 | GOF voltage-gated channel/decreased | 120 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R303 | GOF voltage-gated channel/increased | 9 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R304 | LOF catalytic activity/increased | 86 | 2 | 0 | 1 |
| R305 | LOF catalytic activity/decreased | 464 | 2 | 0 | 1 |

R304 is not supported by its matched control: the same two training hits and
the same contraindication occur when catalytic activity is decreased, and the
control has much broader coverage. Its remaining predictions are
context-dependent enzyme activity statements rather than clear restoration.
R304 is rejected with R303 and R305.

R302 has no visible calibration evidence but is retained as an uncalibrated,
high-mechanism exploratory family. Partition-safe examples include Diltiazem
for Timothy syndrome through GOF CACNA1C, several calcium-channel blockers for
Timothy syndrome or a CACNA1C-related adenoma syndrome, Ziprasidone for short
QT syndrome 1, and Amifampridine for short QT syndrome. These are predictions,
not validated discoveries.

### Bounded provenance audit

The following identity query was restricted to two examples already classified
as target-free by the evaluator:

```cypher
UNWIND [
  {drug: 'CHEBI:101278', disease: 'MONDO:0010979'},
  {drug: 'CHEBI:10119', disease: 'MONDO:0012312'}
] AS pair
MATCH (subject:Drug {id: pair.drug})
      -[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
      -[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease {id: pair.disease})
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[:ENABLES]->(activity:MolecularActivity)
      -[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..]->(:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0022832'})
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'gain_of_function_variant_form'
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id, object.id, driver.id, activity.id,
       binding.primary_knowledge_sources,
       effect.primary_knowledge_sources,
       effect.publications[0..5], association.publications[0..5]
LIMIT 20
```

Diltiazem-Timothy syndrome resolved cleanly through CACNA1C. CACNA1C enables
multiple voltage-gated calcium-channel activities; direct binding is supplied
independently by WOMBAT-PK, DGIdb, and BindingDB; the decreasing-activity edge
comes from the targeted text-mining provider with multiple publications; and
the GOF disease edge has multiple Gene2Phenotype publications. The second pair
did not resolve under this exact audit query. This query used the older
`activity_or_abundance` aspect and narrower `GO:0022832` root, whereas the
compiled rule uses explicit `activity` and `GO:0005244`; the failed trace is
therefore recorded as an audit-query mismatch, not as evidence for or against
the prediction.

Graph limitation: explicit activity direction is still an extraction-level
statement without experimental context, dose, tissue, allele, or proof that
the molecular effect is therapeutic. R302's ontology constraint improves
mechanistic coherence but cannot establish clinical direction by itself.

## Batch 45: Signed Downstream Interception

### Hypothesis and executable family

Earlier signed-chain rules propagated a drug perturbation upstream toward a
causal driver. This family instead asks whether a drug can intercept a signed
downstream consequence of an explicit disease driver:

```cypher
MATCH (driver:Gene)-[regulation:REGULATES]->(effector:Gene)
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (effector)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(effector)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE driver <> effector
  AND 'infores:signor' IN
      coalesce(regulation.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND regulation.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND regulation.object_direction_qualifier IN
      ['upregulated', 'downregulated']
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $composed_direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

For GOF correction, `$composed_direction` opposes the SIGNOR direction. For
LOF correction, it restores the normal SIGNOR direction. Reversing those
assignments creates matched worsening controls. The initial aggregate coverage
probe returned 1,262 GOF-corrective, 776 GOF-worsening, 1,256 LOF-corrective,
and 1,536 LOF-worsening pairs.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T160641Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Mechanism | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R306 | GOF downstream correction | 1,262 | 9 | 0 | 0 |
| R307 | GOF downstream reinforcement | 776 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R308 | LOF downstream restoration | 1,256 | 4 | 2 | 2 |
| R309 | LOF downstream worsening | 1,536 | 0 | 1 | 1 |

R308 and R309 overlap on 395 pairs. R308-only contains all four training and
both DRH hits; R309-only contains its one DRH hit. The broad family is
directionally informative but has only 0.48% visible precision.

### Known-path audit and mechanistic branch split

A bounded query over already classified training and validation examples
returned the driver, effector, signs, binding sources, and publications.
Several paths are genuinely coherent:

- BARD1 LOF is represented as loss of repression of ESR1. Raloxifene,
  Tibolone, and Tamoxifen directly bind ESR1 and decrease its activity or
  abundance. Raloxifene and Tibolone are DRH validation hits and Tamoxifen is
  training.
- TSC1 LOF is represented as loss of repression of MTOR. Everolimus directly
  binds and decreases MTOR and is a training hit for situs inversus.
- The worsening control's DRH hit is Stanozolol-breast cancer: BARD1
  downregulates ESR1, while Stanozolol increases ESR1.

The family was then split by the regulatory branch rather than by a numerical
threshold:

| Rule | Branch | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R310 | GOF upregulated effector, decrease it | 1,134 | 9 | 0 | 0 |
| R311 | GOF downregulated effector, increase it | 150 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R312 | LOF normally upregulated effector, increase it | 308 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R313 | LOF normally downregulated effector, decrease it | 960 | 4 | 2 | 2 |
| R314 | R310 direction control | 229 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R315 | R311 direction control | 566 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R316 | R312 direction control | 1,299 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R317 | R313 direction control | 254 | 0 | 1 | 1 |

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T160908Z/summary.json`.

All visible LOF-corrective evidence is in the disinhibition-suppression branch
R313. R311 and R312 have no visible calibration, while R310 remains a broad,
training-only GOF signal. R306, R308, and R310-R313 are rejected as library
entries after the more specific provenance variant below; R307 and R309 and
R314-R317 remain rejected controls.

### Independent binding provenance

One pre-specified provenance ablation required at least two distinct binding
sources per drug-effector pair:

```cypher
WITH subject, object, collect(DISTINCT binding) AS bindings
WITH subject, object,
     reduce(
       sources = [],
       relation IN bindings |
       reduce(
         accumulated = sources,
         source IN coalesce(relation.primary_knowledge_sources, []) |
         CASE
           WHEN source IN accumulated THEN accumulated
           ELSE accumulated + source
         END
       )
     ) AS binding_sources
WHERE size(binding_sources) >= 2
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T161044Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R318 | LOF disinhibited effector suppression | 436 | 4 | 2 | 1 |
| R319 | matched effector activation control | 128 | 0 | 0 | 1 |

R318 preserves every visible R313 positive while removing 524 unresolved
pairs and one adverse pair. Visible precision rises from 0.625% to 1.376%;
validation precision rises from 0.209% to 0.463%. R319 loses the worsening
control's DRH hit and retains only its adverse pair. No other binding-source
cutoff was tested.

The novel set remains noisy. Examples include potentially coherent
Duvelisib-Cowden syndrome and Bazedoxifene-breast cancer paths, but also
Zileuton-Rett syndrome, Erlotinib-ITCH deficiency, and multiple
neurodevelopmental syndromes connected through broad regulatory effectors.
R318 is retained only as an exploratory high-mechanism family representative,
not as a precision rule.

The accepted-status Pareto surfaces remain unchanged after refresh:

- Global visible: 32.
- Global validation: 9.
- Treatment-free visible: 17.
- Treatment-free validation: 9.

R318 is on none of the four surfaces. The graph limitation is contextual:
SIGNOR establishes a signed regulatory effect, but not whether that effect is
active in the disease-relevant cell, developmental stage, or allele context.
Multi-database binding improves target confidence without solving regulatory
context or effector-hub fan-out.

## Batch 46: Protein Turnover and Secretion Correction

### Qualifier inventory and hypothesis

An aggregate inventory of qualified Drug-to-Gene `AFFECTS` edges identified
mechanistic aspects not used by the earlier activity, abundance, and splicing
families:

| Aspect/direction | Edges |
|---|---:|
| stability increased / decreased | 73 / 48 |
| degradation increased / decreased | 223 / 71 |
| secretion increased / decreased | 1,537 / 750 |

The hypothesis composes these explicit protein-handling directions with
Gene2Phenotype variant direction:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = $aspect
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Corrective assignments were:

- GOF: increased degradation or decreased stability.
- LOF: decreased degradation or increased secretion.

Matched opposite-direction controls were probed. GOF decreased-degradation,
GOF increased-stability, and both LOF stability directions had zero coverage.
The six nonzero modes each produced exactly one pair.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T161756Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Mechanism | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R320 | GOF degradation increased | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R321 | GOF stability decreased | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R322 | LOF degradation decreased | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R323 | LOF degradation increased control | 1 | 0 | 0 | 1 |
| R324 | LOF secretion increased | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R325 | LOF secretion decreased control | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

### Partition-safe predictions and path audit

R320 predicts Sorafenib for pheochromocytoma through GOF RET. CTD asserts
increased RET degradation from PMID 17664273; direct binding is independently
represented by SIGNOR, GtoPdb, DGIdb, ChEMBL, and BindingDB; the RET disease
edge cites PMID 8825918. This is a coherent high-mechanism prediction, though
it has no visible calibration.

R322 predicts Calcitriol for vitamin D-dependent rickets type 2A through LOF
VDR. CTD asserts decreased VDR degradation from PMID 16467308; binding is
represented by TTD, GtoPdb, DGIdb, WOMBAT-PK, ChEMBL, and BindingDB; the VDR
disease edge has multiple publications. This is also a coherent
high-mechanism prediction, with the caveat that preserving a mutant receptor
does not guarantee restored function.

R321 predicts Tolcapone for hereditary systemic amyloidosis 1 through GOF TTR
and a ChEMBL `stability decreased` edge from PMID 36306808. The executable
graph path is exact, but the stability qualifier is semantically ambiguous:
it does not state whether protein stability, aggregate stability, or another
assay endpoint decreases. R321 remains an exploratory candidate with a
specific qualifier-semantics warning.

R323's sole pair is the explicit contraindication Fluorouracil-
dihydropyrimidine dehydrogenase deficiency. This is a strong negative control:
increasing degradation of the LOF DPYD protein is aligned with toxicity, not
treatment.

R324 and R325 both produce Vitamin A-progressive retinal dystrophy due to
retinol transport defect through LOF RBP4. The same CTD publication,
PMID 22001960, supports both increased and decreased RBP4 secretion. Because
the corrective and worsening directions are indistinguishable at the pair and
publication level, R324 is rejected with R325.

R320-R322 remain tiny high-mechanism exploratory candidates. They cannot enter
a metric Pareto surface because none has a visible positive, and their
single-pair coverage is insufficient for empirical precision estimation. No
threshold tuning or additional qualifier gate was performed.

Graph limitations:

- Protein-handling qualifiers lack assay context and may refer to mutant,
  wild-type, aggregate, or compartment-specific stability.
- Direct binding and corrected protein quantity do not establish restored
  function for a LOF allele.
- Contradictory secretion directions can originate from the same publication
  without a condition, tissue, dose, or experimental-system qualifier.

## Batch 47: Inheritance-Aware Expression Correction

### Allelic inventory and hypotheses

Gene2Phenotype edges carry `allelic_requirement`,
`gene2phenotype_confidence_category`, and explicit variant-form qualifiers.
The 3,052 Gene2Phenotype edges include:

- 2,011 LOF, 175 GOF, and 98 dominant-negative variant-form edges.
- 1,945 definitive, 845 strong, and 262 moderate assertions.
- 167 autosomal-dominant GOF edges.
- 95 autosomal-dominant dominant-negative edges.
- 472 autosomal-dominant and 1,389 autosomal-recessive LOF edges.

The graph does not carry a separate haploinsufficiency or residual-function
property. The tested expression hypotheses therefore separated inheritance
and variant mechanism explicitly:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'expression'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND association.allelic_requirement = $inheritance
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Hypotheses:

1. Decrease expression of an autosomal-dominant GOF driver.
2. Decrease expression of an autosomal-dominant dominant-negative driver.
3. Increase expression of an autosomal-dominant LOF driver as a possible
   haploinsufficiency rescue.
4. Increase expression of an autosomal-recessive LOF driver as a diagnostic
   control lacking residual-function evidence.

Each had an opposite-direction control.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T162235Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Allele mechanism / expression direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R326 | AD GOF / decreased | 43 | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| R327 | AD GOF / increased control | 20 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R328 | AD dominant-negative / decreased | 9 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R329 | AD dominant-negative / increased control | 11 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R330 | AD LOF / increased | 9 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R331 | AD LOF / decreased control | 34 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R332 | AR LOF / increased diagnostic | 27 | 0 | 0 | 1 |
| R333 | AR LOF / decreased control | 34 | 0 | 0 | 1 |

R326's training hits are Erlotinib and Gefitinib for lung cancer through AD
GOF EGFR and decreased EGFR expression. Target-free examples include
Sunitinib for FGFR-related syndromes, Amiodarone for Timothy syndrome and long
QT syndrome 3, and Quercetin for MET-related cancer. Some are plausible target
suppression, while the channel examples may be adverse.

R328's nine target-free predictions include:

- Plerixafor-WHIM syndrome 1 through dominant-negative CXCR4 and decreased
  CXCR4 expression; binding is represented by BindingDB, ChEMBL, DGIdb,
  GtoPdb, SIGNOR, and TTD.
- Multiple PPARG ligands for PPARG-related familial partial lipodystrophy.
- Caffeine for aniridia-cerebellar ataxia-intellectual disability syndrome.
- Sunitinib for Hartsfield-Bixler-Demyer syndrome.

R330 includes Liothyronine for THRA-related congenital hypothyroidism and
Dextromethorphan or Ketamine for GRIN2A-related Landau-Kleffner syndrome, but
also Amiodarone-long QT syndrome 2 and Ethanol-epilepsy. The graph does not
identify which allele is expressed, whether the remaining allele is
functional, or whether increased total expression restores dosage. R330 is
rejected.

The autosomal-recessive diagnostic includes the adverse
Fluorouracil-dihydropyrimidine dehydrogenase deficiency pair. This supports
the decision not to interpret generic expression increase as LOF rescue.

### Explicit expression-direction contradiction

Six of R328's nine pairs also occur in the increased-expression control R329;
all six are PPARG-related familial partial lipodystrophy predictions. The same
PPARG ligands have publications in both directions. R326 and R327 overlap on
four pairs, while both R326 training hits are in the decrease-only subset.

The single negative-evidence ablation excluded any drug-driver pair with an
opposite expression edge:

```cypher
AND NOT EXISTS {
  MATCH (subject)-[opposite_effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
  WHERE opposite_effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
    AND opposite_effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'expression'
    AND opposite_effect.object_direction_qualifier = $opposite_direction
}
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T162445Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Directionally unopposed branch | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R334 | AD GOF decreased | 39 | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| R335 | AD dominant-negative decreased | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R336 | AD GOF increased control | 16 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R337 | AD dominant-negative increased control | 5 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

R334 improves visible precision from 4.65% to 5.13% while preserving both
training hits. R335 retains exactly three predictions:

- Plerixafor-WHIM syndrome 1.
- Caffeine-aniridia-cerebellar ataxia-intellectual disability syndrome.
- Sunitinib-Hartsfield-Bixler-Demyer syndrome.

R334 and R335 remain high-mechanism candidates; R326 and R328 are rejected as
broader parents. Neither candidate has DRH calibration or enters a Pareto
surface. R327, R329, R331-R333, R336, and R337 remain controls or rejected
diagnostics.

Graph limitations:

- Drug effects are not allele-specific, so reducing total expression could
  suppress both mutant and normal alleles.
- Autosomal-dominant LOF does not imply haploinsufficiency or a functional
  remaining allele.
- CTD expression direction lacks cell, dose, developmental-stage, and disease
  context; contradictory directions are common for well-studied targets.
- Dominant-negative assignments can be biologically subtle, and an apparent
  expression mechanism may coexist with a more important receptor-antagonist
  or agonist mechanism not represented by the rule.

## Batch 48: Epigenetic-Expression Consensus

Hypothesis: paired effect aspects could be more reliable than either edge
alone. For a directly bound LOF driver, decreased methylation plus increased
expression was treated as a putative dosage-restoration mechanism:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[methylation:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (subject)-[expression:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE methylation.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND methylation.object_aspect_qualifier = 'methylation'
  AND methylation.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND expression.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND expression.object_aspect_qualifier = 'expression'
  AND expression.object_direction_qualifier = 'increased'
  AND methylation <> expression
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'loss_of_function_variant_form'
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

The LOF corrective mode had one pair. LOF increased-methylation/decreased-
expression, GOF increased-methylation/decreased-expression, and GOF
decreased-methylation/increased-expression controls all had zero coverage.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T162657Z/summary.json`.

R338's sole target-free pair is Valproic acid-GABA aminotransaminase
deficiency through LOF ABAT. A complete effect audit shows:

- decreased ABAT methylation from CTD, PMID 29154799;
- increased ABAT expression from seven CTD publications;
- decreased ABAT expression from CTD, PMID 29501571;
- decreased ABAT activity from TTD and CTD, including PMID 12093128;
- direct binding represented by TTD.

The cross-aspect negative evidence is stronger than the proposed positive
composition: Valproic acid directly decreases activity of the already
LOF-deficient enzyme, and expression direction is contradictory. R338 is
rejected. The family is coverage-blocked after excluding opposite expression
or decreased-activity evidence.

Graph limitation: methylation direction is not linked to a promoter, CpG,
protein site, tissue, or demonstrated expression consequence. Even observing
the expected expression direction does not establish net functional rescue
when activity and opposite-expression edges coexist.

## Batch 49: Functional Activity-Abundance Consensus

Hypothesis: independent increases in both activity and abundance of a directly
bound LOF driver should be stronger evidence of restored functional dosage
than either aspect alone.

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[activity:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (subject)-[abundance:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE activity.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND activity.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND activity.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND abundance.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND abundance.object_aspect_qualifier = 'abundance'
  AND abundance.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND activity <> abundance
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'loss_of_function_variant_form'
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

The GOF both-decreased consensus had zero coverage. A mixed GOF pattern,
decreased activity plus increased abundance, had nine pairs but was not
promoted because the aspects disagree. LOF both-increased and both-decreased
were evaluated.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T162949Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R339 | LOF activity and abundance increased | 3 | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| R340 | LOF activity and abundance decreased | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

R339's training hit is Migalastat-Fabry disease. Its two target-free
predictions are Estradiol and Enzalutamide for androgen insensitivity
syndrome through AR. This gives 33.3% visible precision, but the full effect
audit invalidates a simple consensus interpretation:

- Migalastat has increased GLA abundance and increased GLA activity, but also
  a SIGNOR decreased-activity edge.
- Enzalutamide has increased AR abundance and one increased-activity
  publication, but many independent decreased-activity edges from GtoPdb,
  DGIdb, CTD, and ChEMBL.
- Estradiol has increased AR abundance and activity without a visible
  opposite activity edge, but increasing total mutant-receptor quantity does
  not establish restored androgen signaling.

The matched control's sole prediction is Botulinum toxin type A-congenital
myasthenic syndrome 18, which is not credible treatment evidence.

R339 is rejected despite its favorable small-sample precision because the
known positive and one prediction both have explicit opposite activity
evidence, while the remaining prediction lacks functional-allele evidence.
An opposite-activity veto would remove the only training hit and leave only
Estradiol-androgen insensitivity, so it was not promoted as another rule.

Graph limitation: aspect-level consensus does not imply experimental-context
consensus. Multiple publications can describe opposite activity directions
for the same drug-protein pair under different mutants, doses, tissues, or
assays, none of which are preserved on the normalized edge.

## Batch 50: Dominant-Negative Activity Suppression

Hypothesis: decreasing activity of a directly bound dominant-negative disease
driver could reduce interference by the mutant protein. The opposite activity
direction was evaluated as a matched worsening control.

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'dominant_negative_variant_form'
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

An aggregate coverage probe also tested degradation, stability, and abundance
aspects before selecting activity. No dominant-negative driver had a directly
bound drug with decreased degradation or stability. Decreased abundance had
zero pairs, while increased abundance had three. Activity was the only
adequately populated aspect: 134 decreased-activity pairs and 44
increased-activity pairs.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T163251Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Activity direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R341 | Decreased, proposed correction | 134 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R342 | Increased, worsening control | 44 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

R341 examples include sodium-channel drugs for developmental epileptic
encephalopathy 13, Plerixafor and related CXCR4 antagonists for WHIM syndrome,
Vitamin A for a microphthalmia syndrome, Crisaborole for collagen disorders,
antiandrogens for Kennedy disease, and multiple kinase inhibitors. Several
are mechanistically interesting, especially Plerixafor-WHIM syndrome, but the
family has no visible treatment calibration.

A direction-overlap ablation found 125 R341-only pairs, 35 R342-only pairs,
and nine pairs present in both sets. The intersection contains
Plerixafor-WHIM syndrome, PPARG ligand-lipodystrophy pairs, and
antiandrogen-Kennedy disease pairs. Thus, some of the most plausible examples
also carry explicit activity evidence in the nominally worsening direction.

R341 is rejected rather than retained as an uncalibrated candidate. Its broad
134-pair output has zero training or DRH hits, the matched control also has
zero calibration, and the graph cannot distinguish inhibition of a mutant
protein from inhibition of the normal allele. The narrower
expression-direction family R335 remains the better dominant-negative
hypothesis because it explicitly excludes opposite expression evidence and
returns only three pairs.

Graph limitations:

- Activity edges are not allele-specific and do not identify mutant versus
  wild-type protein.
- Opposite activity directions commonly coexist across assays, doses,
  tissues, and variants that are collapsed onto one normalized drug-gene pair.
- The dominant-negative qualifier is attached to the gene-disease
  association, not to a drug-responsive molecular species.
- Absence of visible treatment hits cannot distinguish a genuinely novel
  mechanism from a systematically unsupported family while TTD remains blind.

## Batch 51: Activity-Phosphorylation Consensus

Hypothesis: a drug effect on a causal driver is more credible when functional
activity and phosphorylation move in the same allele-corrective direction.
GOF correction requires both aspects decreased; LOF correction requires both
increased. Discordant and worsening branches were evaluated as controls.

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[activity:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (subject)-[phosphorylation:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE activity.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND activity.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND activity.object_direction_qualifier = $activity_direction
  AND phosphorylation.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND phosphorylation.object_aspect_qualifier = 'phosphorylation'
  AND phosphorylation.object_direction_qualifier =
      $phosphorylation_direction
  AND activity <> phosphorylation
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

All phosphorylation-effect edges in the graph are CTD-derived: 13,164
increased, 8,549 decreased, and 3,865 without direction. Thus, this is
agreement between two molecular aspects, not independent phosphorylation
databases.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T163820Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Allele / activity / phosphorylation | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R343 | GOF / decreased / decreased | 40 | 3 | 0 | 0 |
| R344 | GOF / decreased / increased control | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R345 | LOF / increased / increased | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R346 | LOF / decreased / decreased control | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

R343's three training hits are Erlotinib, Gefitinib, and Afatinib for lung
cancer through GOF EGFR. Exact path audits show abundant direct inhibitor
evidence from TTD, GtoPdb, DGIdb, ChEMBL, SIGNOR, and CTD, while the
phosphorylation edges are CTD-derived.

Mechanistically coherent target-free examples include:

- Infigratinib-achondroplasia through GOF FGFR3.
- Vandetanib-pheochromocytoma through GOF RET.
- Sunitinib or Imatinib-infantile myofibromatosis through GOF PDGFRB.
- Sorafenib-cardiofaciocutaneous syndrome 3 and Noonan syndrome 13 through
  GOF MAPK-pathway drivers.

The family also expands one kinase inhibitor across multiple rare syndromes
sharing the same causal gene, so pair count overstates independent molecular
mechanisms.

R344 contains four target-free pairs. Two overlap R343 because the graph has
both phosphorylation directions: Sorafenib-Noonan syndrome 13 and
Everolimus-Proteus syndrome. The other discordant examples include
Capivasertib-Proteus syndrome and Quercetin-MTOR-pathway overgrowth syndrome.

The sole negative-evidence ablation excluded an opposite increased-
phosphorylation edge:

```cypher
AND NOT EXISTS {
  MATCH (subject)-[opposite:AFFECTS]->(driver)
  WHERE opposite.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
    AND opposite.object_aspect_qualifier = 'phosphorylation'
    AND opposite.object_direction_qualifier = 'increased'
}
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T164116Z/summary.json`.

R347 retains 38 pairs and all three training hits, improving visible precision
from 7.50% to 7.89%. No R343 pair has opposite increased-activity evidence.
R347 is retained as the clean high-mechanism GOF variant; R343 is rejected as
its broader parent.

R345's three target-free examples are:

- Calcitriol-vitamin D-dependent rickets type 2A through LOF VDR.
- Testosterone-androgen insensitivity syndrome through LOF AR.
- Human insulin-Donohue syndrome through LOF INSR.

These are coherent receptor-agonist or replacement concepts, but increasing
wild-type activity and phosphorylation does not establish rescue of a mutant
receptor. R345 remains an exploratory candidate with no visible calibration.
R346's worsening examples are Sorafenib-piebaldism and Ponatinib-piebaldism or
hypogonadotropic hypogonadism, supporting the sign distinction but not
calibrating precision.

Graph limitations:

- Phosphorylation direction is not tied to a residue, kinase, phosphatase,
  activating versus inhibitory site, tissue, dose, or mutant allele.
- All phosphorylation evidence comes from one source, CTD.
- A drug can have both phosphorylation directions across collapsed contexts.
- Multiple disease pairs can be generated by one drug-gene mechanism, so
  nominal pair counts are not independent observations.

## Batch 52: Proteostasis and Trafficking Rescue Probe

Hypothesis: direct pharmacological-chaperone-like effects on folding,
stability, localization, or transport of an explicit LOF driver could restore
functional protein in a way not captured by generic activity edges.

The aggregate executable probe was:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'loss_of_function_variant_form'
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN effect.object_aspect_qualifier AS aspect,
       effect.object_direction_qualifier AS direction,
       count(DISTINCT [subject.id, object.id]) AS pairs
ORDER BY pairs
```

No folding-direction pair reached an LOF disease driver. The only potentially
corrective coverage was one increased-localization pair and two
increased-transport pairs; decreased localization had zero LOF pairs and
decreased transport had one. The graph globally contains 115 undirected
folding edges, three increased-folding edges, and eight decreased-folding
edges, but none intersects this causal-driver motif.

No candidate rule was promoted. `localization increased` does not state the
destination compartment, and `transport increased` does not state inward
versus outward transport, membrane delivery, or restoration of the mutant
protein. This family is graph-blocked rather than threshold-blocked.

Graph gap: a useful pharmacological-chaperone rule needs protein species,
mutant allele, subcellular origin and destination, folding state, degradation
or surface-delivery consequence, and preferably a rescue assay. The current
normalized drug-gene effect edge carries none of those linked contexts.

## Batch 53: Allele-Directed Shared-Driver Phenotype Transfer

Hypothesis: indication transfer should be highly specific when a non-blind
treated anchor and candidate disease share a phenotype, the same causal gene,
the same Gene2Phenotype variant mechanism, and a direct allele-corrective drug
effect.

The fully allele-matched form was:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[treatment:TREATS|AMELIORATES_CONDITION]->
      (anchor:Disease)
WHERE any(
  source IN coalesce(treatment.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  WHERE NOT source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
)
MATCH (subject)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[anchor_association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(anchor)
MATCH (driver)-[candidate_association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (anchor)-[:HAS_PHENOTYPE]->(phenotype:PhenotypicFeature)
      <-[:HAS_PHENOTYPE]-(object)
WHERE anchor <> object
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND anchor_association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      candidate_association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(anchor_association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(candidate_association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND phenotype._miner_disease_member_degree <= 34
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Both explicit-activity and broad `activity_or_abundance` forms had zero
coverage. Treated anchors rarely have a Gene2Phenotype association carrying
the same variant qualifier as the candidate.

R348 relaxed only the anchor side to the aggregate
`MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE` relation while preserving explicit GOF
direction on the candidate:

```cypher
MATCH (driver)-[:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(anchor)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'gain_of_function_variant_form'
```

The LOF-corrective and GOF-worsening aggregate controls had zero coverage.
Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T164521Z/summary.json`.

R348 returns one pair, one training hit, no DRH hit, and no prediction:
Fx-1006A/Tafamidis-hereditary systemic amyloidosis 1. The treated anchor is
familial amyloid neuropathy; both diseases involve TTR and share orthostatic
hypotension due to autonomic dysfunction, a phenotype occurring in 15
diseases. The decreased-TTR activity evidence is represented by DGIdb and
ChEMBL.

Removing phenotype specificity adds only the generic muscle-weakness path for
the same pair and creates no additional candidate. R348 is rejected despite
100% visible precision because it has zero prediction capacity. It is a
successful reconstruction of a known TTR mechanism, not a useful mined rule.

Graph limitation: variant-qualified rare-disease associations and broad
treated-disease evidence occupy poorly overlapping parts of the graph. A
shared causal gene and phenotype can become so restrictive that the rule
merely restates an existing indication.

## Batch 54: Allele Direction Plus Disease-Relative Literature

Hypothesis: disease-relative literature relevance may select the important
driver in a noisy disease without losing explicit GOF or LOF correction. This
combines an independently derived literature feature with direct activity
direction and Gene2Phenotype variant mechanism.

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (driver)-[evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND evidence._miner_causal_publication_count >= 3
  AND evidence._miner_causal_publication_npmi >= 0.5
  AND evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T165345Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Variant / direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R349 | GOF / decreased | 34 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R350 | GOF / increased control | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R351 | LOF / increased | 43 | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| R352 | LOF / decreased control | 88 | 2 | 0 | 0 |

R349 has no visible calibration, but its target-free predictions are
concentrated in coherent GOF kinase and RAS syndromes rather than diffuse
common-disease hubs. Exact audits include:

- Infigratinib-achondroplasia through GOF FGFR3, disease rank 1, 24 causal
  publications, NPMI 0.808, and activity evidence from GtoPdb, DrugCentral,
  DGIdb, CTD, and ChEMBL.
- Lonafarnib-Costello syndrome through GOF HRAS, disease rank 1, 13 causal
  publications, and NPMI 0.916.
- Lorlatinib-fibrodysplasia ossificans progressiva through GOF ACVR1, disease
  rank 1, six causal publications, NPMI 1.0, and a ChEMBL activity
  publication.
- Multiple FGFR inhibitors for achondroplasia, hypochondroplasia, and Crouzon
  syndrome.

The only R350 worsening-control pair is Palifermin-Crouzon syndrome, a
directionally credible adverse control because Palifermin activates FGFR
signaling while the disease driver is GOF. R349 remains an uncalibrated
high-mechanism candidate because the 34:1 directional separation and audited
paths are strong, but its precision cannot be estimated from visible targets.

R351's training hits are Migalastat-Fabry disease and
Sapropterin-phenylketonuria. The matched worsening control contains
Migalastat-Fabry and Miglustat-glycogen storage disease II. Five pairs occur
in both directions; the overlap includes Migalastat-Fabry,
Imatinib-piebaldism, and antiandrogen-androgen-insensitivity pairs.

The R351-only subset has 38 pairs, only one training hit, and no validation
hit. Its predictions include numerous dopamine-receptor drugs projected to
adrenoleukodystrophy, hormones projected to androgen insensitivity, and
incretin drugs projected to hypophosphatasia. These reflect drug-target
activity on a relevant gene, not demonstrated restoration of the mutant
protein. R351 is rejected; R352 remains a rejected control.

Graph limitation: literature concentration estimates gene-disease relevance,
not whether a normalized drug effect applies to the pathogenic allele,
tissue, dose, or assay context. It can prioritize a causal driver but cannot
repair ambiguous pharmacologic direction.

## Batch 55: GOF Prenylation Suppression

Hypothesis: decreased prenylation is a direct post-translational correction
for a prenylation-dependent GOF driver because it can prevent membrane
localization and downstream signaling.

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'prenylation'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'gain_of_function_variant_form'
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

The increased-prenylation matched control has zero coverage. Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T165650Z/summary.json`.

R353 returns two target-free predictions, no visible hits, and no adverse
pair:

- Simvastatin-Noonan syndrome 3.
- Simvastatin-cardiofaciocutaneous syndrome 2.

Both paths use GOF KRAS and the same CTD decreased-prenylation assertion,
PMID 8063617. Thus, the two disease pairs are one molecular hypothesis, not
independent evidence. The mechanism is nevertheless substantially more
specific than a generic decreased-activity edge: it identifies the exact
post-translational dependency being perturbed.

R353 remains an uncalibrated high-mechanism candidate. It should be reported
