It produced 11,365 decreased-effect pairs and 4,682 increased-effect pairs.
Requiring a distinct top-three disease driver in the same process reduced those
counts to 935 and 200, still too broad. Crossing the targeted gene itself with
an explicit Gene2Phenotype GOF or LOF association returned no rows.

### Top-three target plus independent disease-process support

R404-R405 use a more conservative architecture:

```cypher
MATCH (process:Pathway)-[path_evidence:GENETIC_ASSOCIATION]->
      (object:Disease)
MATCH (target:Gene)-[membership:INVOLVED_IN]->(process)
MATCH (target)-[gene_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (target)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
WHERE process.id STARTS WITH 'GO:'
  AND 'infores:geneticskp' IN
      coalesce(path_evidence.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:goa' IN coalesce(membership.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND gene_evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T190123Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R404 | decreased | 45 | 13 | 0 | 2 | 28.89% |
| R405 | increased control | 10 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 10.00% |

R404 training examples include Tacrine-Alzheimer disease, Prednisolone for
asthma, Crohn disease, inflammatory bowel disease, multiple sclerosis, and
rheumatoid arthritis, anti-VEGF drugs for diabetic retinopathy, and
Infliximab for Crohn disease, inflammatory bowel disease, and rheumatoid
arthritis.

Safe novel examples show strong target cohorts:

- TNF inhibition by Infliximab, Lenalidomide, and Pomalidomide across multiple
  inflammatory diseases.
- APP inhibition by Tretinoin, Quercetin, Curcumin, and Methylthioninium for
  Alzheimer disease.
- VEGF-pathway inhibition for diabetic retinopathy.

R404 is retained as a medium-mechanism candidate because it adds an independent
GeneticsKP disease-process assertion and outperforms its direction control.
However, it has no DRH validation hit, contains two heart-failure
contraindications, and is dominated by TNF, APP, and VEGF target cohorts.
R405 is rejected.

Graph limitation: GeneticsKP pathway associations are unsigned and PathBank,
Reactome, and GO pathway identifiers are not integrated. The useful GO subset
cannot establish whether decreasing a pathway member restores or worsens the
disease process.

## Batch 75: Anatomy-to-Cell Composition Bridge

Hypothesis: Bgee cell expression can be connected to disease anatomy through
explicit anatomy-`HAS_PART`-cell assertions, avoiding the invalid Cell-to-Cell
`SUBCLASS_OF` bridge found earlier.

SemMedDB contains 3,722 anatomy-to-cell `HAS_PART` edges. Identifier coverage
includes 2,242 UBERON-to-CL and 901 UMLS-to-CL edges. The unrestricted
top-three target query returned 705 decreased-effect pairs and 66
increased-effect pairs. A separate all-path coverage query was stopped after
60 seconds.

Adding explicit Gene2Phenotype direction produced:

| Branch | Pairs |
|---|---:|
| GOF corrective decrease | 37 |
| GOF worsening increase | 1 |
| LOF corrective increase | 2 |
| LOF worsening decrease | 2 |

R406-R409 execute:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (target:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
MATCH (target)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (target)-[expression:EXPRESSED_IN]->(cell:Cell)
MATCH (anatomy:AnatomicalEntity)-[part:HAS_PART]->(cell)
MATCH (object)-[:LOCATED_IN|OCCURS_IN]->(anatomy)
WHERE 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier = $variant_form
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND 'infores:bgee' IN coalesce(expression.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:semmeddb' IN coalesce(part.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T190608Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Branch | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R406 | GOF corrective | 37 | 8 | 0 | 0 |
| R407 | GOF worsening | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R408 | LOF corrective | 2 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R409 | LOF worsening | 2 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

All eight R406 training hits are one lung-cancer cohort. Most novel predictions
are additional EGFR, ERBB2, or ALK inhibitors for lung cancer; the other cohort
is ACVR1 inhibition for fibrodysplasia ossificans progressiva.

Exact paths expose invalid localization:

- Dacomitinib-lung cancer can be supported by ERBB2 in granulocytes placed in
  blood, liver, or an `Alveolar` UMLS anatomy.
- EGFR can be expressed in pancreatic ductal cells or endometrial stromal cells
  connected to generic epithelium or tissue.
- Metformin-Leigh uses NDUFS4 expression in leukocytes, a literature-mined
  leukocyte-to-brain part relation, and contradictory increased and decreased
  effects.
- Sunitinib-Sturge-Weber uses STK11 in endothelial cells or leukocytes linked
  to camera-type eye and also has both increased and decreased effects.
- Quercetin-lung cancer occurs in both R406 and R407; Metformin-Leigh occurs in
  both R408 and R409.

R406 and R408 are rejected despite the favorable GOF pair count. R406 is
empirically dominated by the earlier direct disease-cell rule R243, and the
SemMedDB partonomy does not reliably localize the target to the disease tissue.
R407 and R409 remain rejected controls.

Graph limitation: a literature assertion that an anatomy has a cell as a part
is not equivalent to the disease occurring in the same cellular compartment.
The graph needs ontology-native cell-to-organ mappings, tissue-specific
expression, and disease-cell localization rather than generic literature
co-mention partonomy.

## Batch 76: Side-Effect Manifestations as Negative Evidence

Hypothesis: a drug should be excluded when a SIDER side effect is explicitly a
SemMedDB `MANIFESTATION_OF` the candidate disease. This is more disease-specific
than exact phenotype overlap.

Coverage is dense:

| Manifestation prefix | Drug-disease pairs | Drugs | Diseases |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| MONDO | 26,663 | 989 | 169 |
| HP | 17,624 | 977 | 65 |
| NCIT | 3,759 | 191 | 41 |
| UMLS | 1,829 | 555 | 45 |
| EFO | 437 | 163 | 10 |

Every side-effect edge is from SIDER and every manifestation edge is from
SemMedDB. Pair-level overlap is large:

| Parent | Parent pairs | Pairs with manifested side effect |
|---|---:|---:|
| R264 | 27 | 11 |
| R268 | 22 | 9 |
| R270 | 16 | 8 |

R410-R412 add this exclusion after target aggregation:

```cypher
WITH subject, object, count(DISTINCT gene) AS targeted_genes
WHERE targeted_genes >= $minimum_targets
  AND NOT EXISTS {
    MATCH (subject)-[:HAS_SIDE_EFFECT]->(:NamedThing)
          -[:MANIFESTATION_OF]->(object)
  }
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T190948Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Parent | Pairs | Training | DRH | Visible precision | Validation precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R410 | R264 | 16 | 3 | 1 | 25.00% | 7.69% |
| R411 | R268 | 13 | 3 | 1 | 30.77% | 10.00% |
| R412 | R270 | 8 | 2 | 1 | 37.50% | 16.67% |

The R270-to-R412 comparator shows that the veto removes eight pairs: five
training treatments and three novel predictions, while removing no DRH hit.
Removed training pairs include Imatinib-CML and Sunitinib, Imatinib, Nilotinib,
and Regorafenib for plexosarcoma. Removed novel predictions include Sorafenib,
Axitinib, and Ponatinib for plexosarcoma.

Exact adverse paths explain the failure:

- Imatinib has SIDER `Blast Phase`, which SemMedDB calls a manifestation of
  BCR-ABL1-positive CML. This is disease progression or indication context, not
  evidence that Imatinib should be excluded.
- Imatinib has the generic side effect `neoplasm`, which SemMedDB links as a
  manifestation of plexosarcoma.
- Gastrointestinal hemorrhage is also linked as a manifestation of
  plexosarcoma through literature co-mention.

R410-R412 are rejected. Their apparent validation-precision increase is caused
by preserving the same DRH hit while deleting established treatments, not by a
valid adverse mechanism.

Graph limitation: `MANIFESTATION_OF` does not distinguish disease progression,
indication-associated findings, treatment-emergent toxicity, or generic
symptoms. A usable adverse gate needs event timing, causal attribution, and
drug-exposure context.

## Batch 77: GeneticsKP Molecular-Activity Corroboration

Hypothesis: an unsigned GeneticsKP disease association to a GO molecular
activity may independently corroborate a disease gene when GOA says that gene
enables the activity. A drug that directly binds and decreases a top-three
disease gene would then have a four-edge mechanism:

```cypher
MATCH (activity:MolecularActivity)-[activity_evidence:GENETIC_ASSOCIATION]->
      (object:Disease)
MATCH (target:Gene)-[enables:ENABLES]->(activity)
MATCH (target)-[gene_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (target)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
WHERE 'infores:geneticskp' IN
      coalesce(activity_evidence.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:goa' IN coalesce(enables.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND gene_evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Graph coverage before drug joins is 1,536 GO molecular activities, 76 diseases,
47,309 genes, and 893,405 gene-activity-disease paths. The drug join produces
75 decreased pairs across 25 activities and eight targets, versus ten increased
control pairs across nine activities and three targets. Intersecting this
architecture with explicit Gene2Phenotype gain- or loss-of-function direction
produces zero pairs.

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T191303Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R413 | decreased | 75 | 13 | 0 | 3 | 17.33% |
| R414 | increased control | 10 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 10.00% |

R413 training examples include Captopril-hypertension through REN peptidase
activity, Prednisolone and Infliximab inflammatory-disease pairs through TNF
activities, and Tacrine-Alzheimer disease through APP binding activities.
Novel examples include Sunitinib-Parkinson disease through LRRK2 kinase or
clathrin binding, but are dominated by KCNH2 inhibitors predicted for long QT
syndrome.

The exact-path probe was restricted to pairs already classified as training or
novel by the blind-safe inspector:

```cypher
UNWIND $pairs AS pair
MATCH (subject:Drug {id: pair.subject})
      -[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (target:Gene)-[enables:ENABLES]->(activity:MolecularActivity)
      -[activity_evidence:GENETIC_ASSOCIATION]->
      (object:Disease {id: pair.object})
MATCH (target)-[gene_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = $causes
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = $aspect
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier IN $directions
  AND $geneticskp IN
      coalesce(activity_evidence.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND $goa IN coalesce(enables.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
RETURN subject.id, object.id, target.id, target.name,
       gene_evidence._miner_disease_rank,
       activity.id, activity.name, effect.object_direction_qualifier,
       binding.primary_knowledge_sources, effect.primary_knowledge_sources
```

Exact paths expose the failure:

- Dofetilide-long QT is explicitly contraindicated. The rule interprets
  decreased KCNH2 delayed-rectifier channel activity as treatment even though
  that pharmacology causes the phenotype. The same unsafe branch generates
  Amiodarone, Cisapride, Erythromycin, Ondansetron, Pimozide, Terfenadine, and
  many other long-QT liabilities.
- Tacrine-APP-Alzheimer disease occurs in both R413 and the increased control
  R414 because the targeted text-mining provider supplies contradictory
  directions for the same drug-target pair.
- Several GeneticsKP activities are generic, including identical protein
  binding, protein-containing complex binding, protease binding, and scaffold
  protein binding. Their disease association does not specify whether activity
  should increase or decrease.
- The plausible Sunitinib-LRRK2-Parkinson path remains unsigned at the disease
  activity edge and therefore cannot establish that LRRK2 inhibition is
  corrective.

An ablation against R404, the GeneticsKP biological-process corroboration rule,
shows:

| Relation | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R404 only | 15 | 3 | 0 | 2 |
| intersection | 30 | 10 | 0 | 0 |
| R413 only | 45 | 3 | 0 | 3 |

Thus most of R413's training signal overlaps the pathway rule, whereas its
larger novel-only branch contains all three contraindications. R413 and R414
are rejected.

Two diagnostic failures were also observed and corrected. The first exact-path
command accidentally embedded terminal ANSI color escapes around Cypher string
literals and failed with `CypherSyntaxError`; the successful query parameterized
all literals. The first comparator command used `--left/--right`, while the CLI
requires `--parent/--child`; the corrected blind-safe comparison is reported
above.

Graph limitation: GeneticsKP disease-to-GO associations are unsigned, and GOA
`ENABLES` states gene function rather than pathological direction. A useful
activity rule needs variant-specific direction or disease-state activity,
preferably linked to tissue and cell context. Text-mined drug effects also need
conflict resolution before direction can be trusted.

## Batch 78: GO-CAM Temporal Gene Chains

Hypothesis: a GO-CAM `PRECEDES` edge may orient a disease driver and a
downstream drug target even when the edge lacks an activation or inhibition
sign. Both edge orientations and both drug-effect directions were tested.

Aggregate executable probe:

```cypher
MATCH (left:Gene)-[pre:PRECEDES]->(right:Gene)
WHERE 'infores:go-cam' IN coalesce(pre.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
WITH CASE WHEN $orientation = 'forward' THEN left ELSE right END AS driver,
     CASE WHEN $orientation = 'forward' THEN right ELSE left END AS target
MATCH (driver)-[evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(target)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN count(DISTINCT subject.id + '|' + object.id) AS pairs,
       count(DISTINCT subject) AS drugs,
       count(DISTINCT object) AS diseases,
       count(DISTINCT driver) AS drivers,
       count(DISTINCT target) AS targets
```

All four branches have zero coverage:

| PRECEDES orientation | Drug direction | Pairs |
|---|---|---:|
| forward | decreased | 0 |
| forward | increased | 0 |
| reverse | decreased | 0 |
| reverse | increased | 0 |

The graph contains 14,602 `PRECEDES` edges, but 14,555 are heterogeneous
SemMedDB assertions. Only 47 are GO-CAM; 34 connect one NCBI Gene node to
another. Examples include `Alox12 -> Hpgd`, `Vamp2 -> Snap25`, and
`Edn1 -> Ednrb`. None joins a top-three disease-ranked driver to a drug with a
usable directional target effect, in either orientation.

No executable catalog rules were added. This family is blocked by sparse
GO-CAM coverage rather than a threshold choice, so work moved immediately to a
different temporal hypothesis.

Graph limitation: the graph needs substantially broader causal temporal
pathway coverage, plus activation/inhibition sign and biological context on
sequence edges. SemMedDB literature order is too heterogeneous to substitute
for GO-CAM causal order at the gene level.

## Batch 79: SemMedDB Temporal Indication Transfer

Hypothesis: if a drug's established indication precedes another disease and
both diseases have the same top-three inhibited causal gene, the drug may
prevent or treat the later disease. Forward versus reverse temporal order and
decreased versus increased drug effect were evaluated as a two-by-two ablation.

The first graph-wide formulation matched all disease temporal edges before
restricting to treated anchors. It ran for more than one minute and was
interrupted. The successful executable family stages the small
drug-anchor-gene set before traversing `PRECEDES`:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[treatment:TREATS|AMELIORATES_CONDITION]->
      (anchor:Disease)
WHERE any(
  source IN coalesce(treatment.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  WHERE NOT source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
)
MATCH (subject)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(gene:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(gene)
MATCH (gene)-[anchor_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(anchor)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND anchor_evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
WITH DISTINCT subject, anchor, gene
MATCH (anchor)-[temporal:PRECEDES]->(object:Disease)
WHERE 'infores:semmeddb' IN
      coalesce(temporal.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
MATCH (gene)-[object_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
WHERE object_evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

R417 and R418 reverse the temporal pattern to
`(object)-[:PRECEDES]->(anchor)`. R416 and R418 change the drug direction to
increased.

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T192136Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Temporal order | Drug direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Visible precision |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R415 | anchor before candidate | decreased | 12 | 7 | 0 | 58.33% |
| R416 | anchor before candidate | increased | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0.00% |
| R417 | candidate before anchor | decreased | 7 | 6 | 0 | 85.71% |
| R418 | candidate before anchor | increased | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0.00% |

The direction control supports target inhibition, but the temporal control
fails decisively: reverse order is more precise than the hypothesized forward
order. R415 and R417 overlap on four pairs, all training treatments. R415 has
eight forward-only pairs with three training hits and five unresolved
predictions; R417 has three reverse-only pairs with two training hits and one
unresolved prediction.

Forward training examples are dominated by Prednisolone and Infliximab through
TNF across asthma, psoriasis, arthritis, and uveitis. Forward novel predictions
are:

- Tacrine-Alzheimer disease-APP to dementia.
- Hydroxychloroquine-COVID-19-ACE2 to pneumonia.
- Prednisolone-pneumonia-TNF to septic shock, meningitis, and adult ARDS.

The latter three do not form a credible therapeutic class and may represent
infection progression or treatment context rather than preventable shared
molecular disease.

Exact blind-safe paths show why the reverse control succeeds:

- `psoriasis PRECEDES arthritis` and `arthritis PRECEDES psoriasis` both occur.
- `arthritis PRECEDES uveitis`, while uveitis also serves as an anchor after
  arthritis in other paths.
- Prednisolone-asthma predicts pneumonia in reverse order, while
  Prednisolone-pneumonia predicts asthma, meningitis, septic shock, and ARDS in
  forward order.
- Aflibercept's reverse training hit is diabetic retinopathy preceding the
  broad anchor `retinal disease`, with VEGFA as the shared gene.
- Tacrine's reverse novel output is amnesia preceding Alzheimer disease; its
  forward output is Alzheimer disease preceding the broader term dementia.

Across all SemMedDB Disease-to-Disease `PRECEDES` edges, there are 2,092 unique
directed pairs. Of these, 209 have an explicit reverse edge, and one disease has
a self-loop. Thus 10% of directed disease pairs are directly reciprocal before
considering broader semantic redundancy.

Operational failures and safeguards:

- The naïve all-pairs query was interrupted after exceeding one minute; staging
  the treated-anchor set reduced each variant to under 0.2 seconds.
- A catalog smoke test appeared to load only R415 because the default loader
  omits rules already marked `rejected`. Explicit ID selection correctly loaded
  R415-R418; the TOML was never malformed.
- The first exact-path audit was rejected before execution because it omitted
  holdout-source exclusions on anchor treatment and target edges. The corrected
  query preserved all TTD/DRH exclusions and only used output pairs already
  classified as training or novel by the blind-safe inspector.
- The combined exact-path query was duplicate-heavy because each treatment and
  binding source generated a row. Pair-level conclusions above are deduplicated.

R415-R418 are rejected. The apparent forward precision is not attributable to
temporal direction, and no DRH validation hit is recovered.

Graph limitation: SemMedDB `PRECEDES` conflates clinical progression,
co-occurring chronology, broad-versus-specific disease order, and literature
statement direction. A useful temporal rule needs event-level patient
trajectories, explicit progression or complication semantics, temporal
confidence, and non-reciprocal direction.

## Batch 80: Pathogen-Target and Organism-Level Rules

Hypothesis: a treatment may act on a pathogen gene belonging to an organism
that causes the disease, providing an infection-specific mechanism outside the
human disease-gene search space.

Schema inventory found 4,958 `NCBITaxon -> MONDO` `CAUSES` edges. All pathogen
etiology edges are SemMedDB-derived. Gene taxon annotations cover 124 taxa;
1,015 disease-causing taxa are represented as organism nodes. Their
intersection contains 24 taxa and 37 genes, including HIV-1, Epstein-Barr
virus, West Nile virus, Zika virus, monkeypox virus, *S. aureus*, *E. coli*,
*P. aeruginosa*, *B. anthracis*, *Y. pestis*, and several parasites.

The intended executable motif was:

```cypher
MATCH (gene:Gene)
WHERE any(
  taxon_id IN coalesce(gene.in_taxon, [])
  WHERE taxon_id IN $disease_causing_gene_taxa
)
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(gene)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = $direction
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
UNWIND [
  taxon_id IN coalesce(gene.in_taxon, [])
  WHERE taxon_id IN $disease_causing_gene_taxa
] AS taxon_id
MATCH (pathogen:Entity {id: taxon_id})-[etiology:CAUSES]->
      (object:Disease)
WHERE 'infores:semmeddb' IN
      coalesce(etiology.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

Both decreased and increased branches produce zero pairs. Staged ablation
locates the missing layer:

| Stage | Covered pathogen genes |
|---|---:|
| Taxon causes disease and annotates gene | 37 |
| Any direct drug-binding edge | 10 |
| Qualified activity-or-abundance `AFFECTS` edge | 0 |
| Both binding and qualified effect | 0 |

The initial drug-first query and a subsequent unlabeled taxon join each timed
out after 30 seconds. Neo4j has one `:Entity(id)` index; matching a taxon without
the `Entity` label prevented index use. The successful strategy independently
collected disease-causing taxon IDs and gene taxon values, intersected the two
graph-derived sets client-side, and restricted the Cypher scan to the 24
overlapping taxa.

Two set-intersection commands failed before execution because Python dictionary
key quotes were over-escaped inside a shell single-quoted command. Corrected
commands produced the coverage above; these were command-construction failures,
not graph results.

An organism-level alternative was also tested:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[action]->(pathogen:OrganismTaxon)
MATCH (pathogen)-[etiology:CAUSES]->(object:Disease)
WHERE 'infores:semmeddb' IN
      coalesce(etiology.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
RETURN type(action), count(DISTINCT subject.id + '|' + object.id)
```

Direct drug-pathogen paths are dominated by non-mechanistic predicates:

| Predicate | Drug-disease pairs | Drugs | Pathogens |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `LOCATED_IN` | 36,980 | 269 | 88 |
| `TREATS_OR_APPLIED_OR_STUDIED_TO_TREAT` | 8,364 | 103 | 44 |
| `INTERACTS_WITH` | 3,641 | 30 | 16 |
| `AFFECTS` | 534 | 21 | 3 |

All 560 direct `AFFECTS` paths are unsigned SemMedDB assertions. Of these, 492
use the broad taxon `Fungi`, 54 use *Saccharomyces cerevisiae*, and 14 use the
UMLS concept `host organism`. None has a qualified predicate, aspect, or
direction. No catalog rule was added.

Chemical-series transfer was considered immediately before this family, but
the live relationship inventory contains no chemical-similarity predicate.
Without graph-contained fingerprints or similarity edges, the rule would
require external information and violate the campaign constraint. Chemical
ontology ancestry was not used as a fake substitute for structural similarity.

Graph limitation: the graph has pathogen etiology and a few microbial genes,
but lacks directional antimicrobial drug effects on those genes and lacks
specific organism-level actions such as killing, growth inhibition, replication
inhibition, or pathogen-abundance decrease. It also lacks chemical fingerprints
or structure-similarity edges for graph-internal analog transfer.

## Batch 81: Disease-Causing Chemical Clearance Activation

Hypothesis: activating an enzyme that consumes a non-drug chemical represented
as causing disease may increase clearance of that chemical. Inhibition of the
same enzyme is the matched worsening control. This differs from the earlier LOF
metabolic-rescue family because no deficient disease enzyme is assumed.

Executable R419:

```cypher
MATCH (enzyme:Gene)-[substrate:HAS_SUBSTRATE]->(chemical:ChemicalEntity)
      -[etiology:CAUSES]->(object:Disease)
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(enzyme)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(enzyme)
WHERE NOT chemical:Drug
  AND subject <> chemical
  AND 'infores:chembl' IN
      coalesce(substrate.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:semmeddb' IN
      coalesce(etiology.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'increased'
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

R420 changes the effect direction to decreased.

Before excluding Drug-labeled intermediates, the activation branch produced
1,635 pairs and the inhibition control produced 13,409. The dominant
"disease-causing chemicals" were methotrexate, cyclosporine, doxorubicin,
cyclophosphamide, dexamethasone, ketamine, and tacrolimus. The principal
enzymes were ABCB1, CYP3A4, ABCC1, CYP2B6, and ABCG2. This represented
drug-drug disposition and toxicity rather than antidote biology.

The semantic `NOT chemical:Drug` ablation reduced activation to 97 pairs across
nine drugs, five enzymes, 14 chemicals, and 43 diseases. The control retained
1,147 pairs across 85 drugs, 22 enzymes, 27 chemicals, and 93 diseases.
Remaining intermediates included aldosterone, corticosterone, stanolone,
chlorpyrifos, glutathione, bilirubin, leukotrienes, SN-38, and endosulfan.

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T193650Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Enzyme direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R419 | increased | 97 | 0 | 0 | 1 |
| R420 | decreased control | 1,147 | 18 | 0 | 33 |

R419 has no positive visible labels. Its contraindicated pair is
Doxorubicin-heart failure. Representative exact paths, restricted to
blind-safe inspected outputs, are:

- Doxorubicin increases ABCB1; aldosterone is an ABCB1 substrate and is linked
  as causing heart failure.
- Nicardipine increases CYP3A4; cibenzoline is a substrate linked as causing
  hypoglycemia.
- Phenytoin increases UGT1A1; bilirubin is a substrate linked as causing
  hyperbilirubinemia.
- Quercetin increases ABCG2; SN-38 is a substrate linked to generic poisoning.
- Trametinib increases ABCB1; stanolone is a substrate linked to androgen
  excess.

Other outputs include Doxorubicin for renal fibrosis, amnesia, cholestasis,
proteinuria, and alopecia; Trametinib for acne and cleft palate; Phenytoin for
kernicterus and cholestasis; and Quercetin for diarrhea and poisoning.

The graph architecture is therefore detecting pharmacokinetic exposure and
adverse-event context. `HAS_SUBSTRATE` does not guarantee that increasing the
gene lowers the substrate in vivo; transport direction, compartment, enzyme
capacity, active metabolites, dose, and induction timing are absent. SemMedDB
chemical `CAUSES` edges also conflate endogenous mediators, drugs, toxicants,
and adverse effects.

R419 and R420 are rejected. No threshold tuning was attempted after the
semantic ablation.

Graph limitation: a clearance rule needs explicit metabolic reaction direction,
substrate-to-product stoichiometry, transporter influx versus efflux, tissue
and compartment, and evidence that the chemical's disease effect is mediated
by accumulation. A generic substrate edge plus literature causation is
insufficient.

## Batch 82: Signed Chemical-Mediator Target Antagonism

Hypothesis: if a chemical causes a disease and increases a directly bound
target, a different drug that directly binds and decreases the same target may
treat the disease. The opposite polarity, in which the mediator decreases the
target and the drug increases it, is a restorative counterpart. Same-direction
drug actions are matched reinforcement controls.

Executable staged query for R421:

```cypher
MATCH (mediator:ChemicalEntity)
      -[mediator_binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (target:Gene)
MATCH (mediator)-[mediator_effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
WHERE mediator_effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND mediator_effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND mediator_effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'increased'
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(mediator_binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(mediator_effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
WITH DISTINCT mediator, target
MATCH (mediator)-[etiology:CAUSES]->(object:Disease)
WHERE 'infores:semmeddb' IN
      coalesce(etiology.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
WITH DISTINCT mediator, target, object
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[drug_binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (target)
MATCH (subject)-[drug_effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
WHERE subject <> mediator
  AND drug_effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND drug_effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND drug_effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(drug_binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(drug_effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

R422 changes mediator/drug directions to decreased/increased. R423 uses
increased/increased and R424 uses decreased/decreased.

A naive all-at-once form exceeded 30 seconds. Staging the distinct
mediator-target and mediator-target-disease relations made all four variants
complete in 6-15 seconds. Before the final drug join:

- Increased mediator-target actions: 1,256 rows, 698 mediators, 596 targets.
- Increased mediator-target-disease triples: 5,786 rows, 266 mediators, 397
  targets, 481 diseases.
- Decreased mediator-target actions: 5,277 rows, 1,971 mediators, 1,250 targets.
- Decreased mediator-target-disease triples: 11,373 rows, 436 mediators, 775
  targets, 580 diseases.

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T194431Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Mediator | Drug | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R421 | increased | decreased | 14,005 | 452 | 16 | 716 | 3.34% |
| R422 | decreased | increased | 9,825 | 281 | 11 | 578 | 2.97% |
| R423 control | increased | increased | 7,104 | 233 | 10 | 440 | 3.42% |
| R424 control | decreased | decreased | 31,616 | 634 | 20 | 1,307 | 2.07% |

The increased/increased control has higher visible and validation precision than
R421. All branches have a contraindication burden larger than their positive
validation signal.

Representative blind-safe positive examples for R421 include
Zafirlukast-asthma, Diltiazem-hypertension, Rufinamide-epilepsy,
Landiolol-arrhythmia, Eslicarbazepine-epilepsy, and Flecainide-ventricular
tachycardia. Contradictory examples include Metoclopramide-tardive dyskinesia,
Atomoxetine-psychosis, Norepinephrine-hypertension, and
Norethisterone-osteoporosis. Novel outputs include Ciprofloxacin for leukemia,
allergy, alopecia, acute myeloid leukemia, and poisoning; Warfarin-diarrhea;
and Yohimbine for a broad cardiovascular, metabolic, neurologic, and
gastrointestinal disease set.

Exact paths, restricted to pairs already exposed through the blind-safe
inspector, show the core problem:

- Etoposide increases TOP2B and is linked to acute myeloid leukemia, while
  Ciprofloxacin is text-mined as decreasing TOP2B.
- Epinephrine or norepinephrine increases ADRA1D/ADRA2A and is linked to
  arrhythmia, while Yohimbine is text-mined as decreasing the same receptors.
- Aripiprazole increases DRD2 and is linked to tardive dyskinesia, while
  contraindicated Metoclopramide is text-mined as decreasing DRD2.
- Dobutamine or dopamine increases ADRB1/ADRB2 and is linked to hypertension,
  while contraindicated Norepinephrine is text-mined as decreasing the same
  receptors.
- In the reinforcement control, Dexamethasone and Isoflurophate both increase
  PTGS2 on a glaucoma path.

The exact-path audit returned many duplicate edge-source combinations because
the graph carries parallel binding assertions. This did not change pair counts,
which are distinct subject-object pairs.

Failures and corrections:

- Four inspector calls initially omitted `--rules rules/campaign-i09.toml` and
  failed before querying Neo4j because the default catalog did not contain
  R421/R423.
- The corrected calls first failed inside the restricted sandbox because local
  Bolt sockets were disallowed. They were rerun with approved localhost access.
- The exact-path query preserved all candidate source exclusions and was
  restricted to already inspected training, validation, or novel pairs. It did
  not inspect unresolved or blind identities.

R421 and R422 are rejected. Their controls show that signed target opposition
does not explain the signal.

Graph limitation: SemMedDB chemical `CAUSES` often represents adverse effects,
drug exposures, or literature association rather than a target-mediated
etiology. The signed target actions are dominated by
`infores:text-mining-provider-targeted`, are context independent, and do not
state receptor agonism/antagonism, dose, tissue, or whether the disease effect
is mediated through that target.

## Batch 83: Genetically Corroborated Mediator Antagonism

Hypothesis: the mediator architecture may become useful if the shared target is
independently among the candidate disease's top three genes. This is a
mechanistic evidence addition rather than a threshold search over the broad
family.

R425-R428 add the following executable clause between the
mediator-target-disease stage and the drug-target stage:

```cypher
MATCH (target)-[gene_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
WHERE gene_evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
```

R425 and R426 retain the opposed directions from R421 and R422. R427 and R428
are the matched same-direction controls.

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T194958Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Mediator | Drug | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R425 | increased | decreased | 112 | 5 | 0 | 24 | 4.46% |
| R426 | decreased | increased | 14 | 0 | 0 | 2 | 0.00% |
| R427 control | increased | increased | 10 | 1 | 0 | 1 | 10.00% |
| R428 control | decreased | decreased | 171 | 14 | 2 | 33 | 9.36% |

R425 versus R428:

| Relation | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R425 only | 7 | 4 | 0 | 0 |
| intersection | 105 | 1 | 0 | 24 |
| R428 only | 66 | 13 | 2 | 9 |

The small R425-only subset is visibly precise but has no held-out validation
and only seven predictions. The shared portion contains nearly all of R425's
contraindications, and the same-direction control owns the validation signal.

R425 training examples are Aripiprazole-ADHD inattentive type,
Cyproterone-prostate cancer, Enzalutamide-prostate cancer,
Nilutamide-prostate cancer, and Sotalol-arrhythmia. Novel examples reveal
strong safety leakage: Amiodarone and Quinidine are predicted for long QT and
torsades de pointes; Cocaine is predicted for arrhythmia; Lacosamide and
Eslicarbazepine are predicted for arrhythmia. Several are explicitly
contraindicated. R428's two DRH examples are Eslicarbazepine-epilepsy and
Quinapril-angioedema, the latter indicating adverse-event rather than
therapeutic signal.

R425 and R426 are rejected. Independent disease-gene support improves
specificity but does not rescue the sign logic and leaves substantial
adverse-event contamination. No additional rank thresholds were tested.

Graph limitation: genetic relevance does not establish the direction in which
a target should be perturbed. The graph needs allele-specific disease
direction, explicit pharmacologic mode of action, causal mediation from the
etiologic chemical through the target, and indication-versus-adverse-event
typing for chemical-disease edges.

## Batch 84: Adverse-Anatomy Negative Evidence

Hypothesis: a positive molecular rule should be discounted when a drug has a
side-effect phenotype localized to an anatomy affected by the candidate
disease. Unlike exact phenotype mimicry, this tests a wider organ-level adverse
mechanism.

Coverage inventory:

- 49,928 drug `HAS_SIDE_EFFECT` edges cover 1,350 distinct phenotypes.
- 487 side-effect phenotypes have 11,489 `LOCATED_IN` phenotype-anatomy pairs
  spanning 2,400 anatomies.
- Disease `LOCATED_IN` covers 66,593 edges, 5,684 diseases, and 3,752
  anatomies; `OCCURS_IN` adds 1,370 edges over 1,356 diseases and 18
  anatomies.
- The staged phenotype-anatomy to disease-anatomy join produces 1,599,293
  paths over 483 phenotypes, 2,047 anatomies, and 5,876 diseases.

The first executable inventory attempted the complete three-way join:

```cypher
MATCH (:Drug)-[:HAS_SIDE_EFFECT]->(p:PhenotypicFeature)
      -[phenotype_location]-(anatomy:AnatomicalEntity)
      <-[:LOCATED_IN|OCCURS_IN]-(disease:Disease)
RETURN type(phenotype_location), count(*)
```

It exceeded one minute and was interrupted without interpreting a result.
Staging distinct side-effect phenotypes and then distinct phenotype-anatomy
pairs completed in 0.4 and 3.1 seconds, respectively.

R429 adds this negative gate to R404:

```cypher
AND NOT EXISTS {
  MATCH (subject)-[:HAS_SIDE_EFFECT]->(:PhenotypicFeature)
        -[:LOCATED_IN]->(adverse_anatomy:AnatomicalEntity)
  MATCH (object)-[:LOCATED_IN|OCCURS_IN]->(adverse_anatomy)
}
```

R430 is the matched diagnostic conflict subset using `EXISTS` instead of
`NOT EXISTS`.

Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T195638Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Subset | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R404 | full pathway rule | 45 | 13 | 0 | 2 | 28.89% |
| R429 | no shared adverse anatomy | 15 | 6 | 0 | 1 | 40.00% |
| R430 | shared adverse anatomy | 30 | 7 | 0 | 1 | 23.33% |

The gate raises lower-bound training precision but removes seven of R404's 13
known treatments and does not concentrate contraindications in the conflict
subset. R429 retains Tacrine-Alzheimer disease, Aflibercept/Faricimab-diabetic
retinopathy, and Infliximab for Crohn disease, inflammatory bowel disease, and
rheumatoid arthritis. Its novel examples include Quercetin/Curcumin-Alzheimer
disease, Infliximab-asthma/type 1 diabetes/multiple sclerosis, and
Luspatercept-diabetic kidney disease/osteoarthritis; it retains the explicit
Infliximab-heart failure contraindication.

R430 removes established Prednisolone treatments for asthma, Crohn disease,
inflammatory bowel disease, multiple sclerosis, and rheumatoid arthritis. It
also removes plausible target-cohort predictions including
Tretinoin/Methylthioninium-Alzheimer disease and anti-TNF candidates across
inflammatory diseases.

An exact-path audit, restricted to pairs already exposed by the blind-safe
inspector, showed why the anti-join is unstable:

- SIDER supplies the side-effect edges.
- SemMedDB supplies both phenotype `LOCATED_IN` and disease `LOCATED_IN`.
- Common side effects such as fatigue, agitation, asthenia, erythema, abdominal
  pain, and arthralgia fan out to brain, eye, kidney, lung, limbs, joints,
  mucosa, blood vessels, and generic organ nodes.
- Lenalidomide-rheumatoid arthritis alone generated hundreds of shared-anatomy
  paths, including biologically implausible links such as agitation to manus
  and abdominal pain to forelimb.

R429 and R430 are rejected. The precision increase is not supported by the
negative-evidence diagnostic and is caused by a broad literature-mined
anatomical anti-join that deletes established therapies.

Graph limitation: useful adverse anatomy requires curated phenotype-to-anatomy
localization, assertion context, anatomical specificity, and a distinction
between the site of a symptom and organs merely mentioned in the same
literature. SemMedDB co-occurrence-derived locations are too expansive for a
hard veto.

## Batch 85: Variant-Directed Ligand-Receptor Axis Correction

Hypothesis: a GOF disease driver with signaling-receptor-binding activity can
be attenuated by suppressing an interacting receptor; a LOF driver can be
compensated by activating an interacting receptor. Opposite drug directions
are matched worsening controls.

Coverage before rule evaluation:

- 6,824 genes enable a descendant of GO signaling receptor activity
  (`GO:0038023`).
- Gene2Phenotype contains 175 GOF edges over 153 drivers and 167 diseases, and
  2,011 LOF edges over 1,928 drivers and 1,935 diseases.
- Requiring a SIGNOR physical interaction to a receptor gives 539 GOF and 688
  LOF driver-receptor-disease paths.
- Requiring the driver to enable a descendant of signaling receptor binding
  (`GO:0005102`) leaves 421 GOF paths over 16 drivers and 467 LOF paths over 78
  drivers.
- Before label partitioning, the four drug-direction cells contained 693 GOF
  decreased, 332 GOF increased, 392 LOF increased, and 902 LOF decreased
  subject-disease pairs.

Executable R431:

```cypher
MATCH (driver:Gene)-[ligand_function:ENABLES]->
      (:MolecularActivity)-[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->
      (:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0005102'})
MATCH (driver)-[interaction:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]-
      (receptor:Gene)
MATCH (receptor)-[receptor_function:ENABLES]->
      (:MolecularActivity)-[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->
      (:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0038023'})
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE driver <> receptor
  AND 'infores:goa' IN
      coalesce(ligand_function.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:signor' IN
      coalesce(interaction.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:goa' IN
      coalesce(receptor_function.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'gain_of_function_variant_form'
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
WITH DISTINCT driver, receptor, object
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (receptor)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(receptor)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

R432 changes the GOF drug direction to increased. R433 uses LOF/increased, and
R434 uses LOF/decreased.

The first implementation joined drugs before deduplicating the two GO closure
paths. R431 and R434 each timed out at 120 seconds, while R432 and R433
completed in 31-33 seconds. Run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T200639Z/summary.json`.

The compiler was rewritten to materialize distinct
driver-receptor-disease triples before joining drugs. During that edit, a
generated `WHERE AND effect...` typo was caught by code inspection before any
query ran and corrected. Targeted tests and Ruff then passed. The staged rules
completed in 5.6-7.9 seconds.

Final run artifact:
`results/runs/20260812T200806Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Variant | Drug direction | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R431 | GOF corrective | decreased | 693 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R432 | GOF control | increased | 332 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R433 | LOF corrective | increased | 392 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R434 | LOF control | decreased | 902 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

All 2,319 outputs are unresolved-or-blind at aggregate level, and safe novel
inspection shows no coherent enrichment. R431 examples include Yohimbine,
Zafirlukast, Zileuton, and Sulfisoxazole for Sturge-Weber syndrome, congenital
hemangioma, McCune-Albright syndrome, and vascular neoplasm; Erlotinib for
Noonan syndrome and CBL-related disorders. R433 examples include Zafirlukast
for Bohring-Opitz and Shashi-Pena syndromes; Zolmitriptan, Ramelteon, Fentanyl,
Istradefylline, and Rotigotine for developmental and epileptic encephalopathy
17; and Rotigotine for pseudopseudohypoparathyroidism.

Exact paths reveal that the assumed ligand role is false:

- GNAQ GOF in Sturge-Weber syndrome has `G protein-coupled receptor binding`
  and interacts with ADRA1D; Yohimbine decreasing ADRA1D is therefore emitted.
- GNAO1 LOF has several receptor-binding annotations and interacts with HTR1B;
  Zolmitriptan increasing HTR1B is emitted for developmental and epileptic
  encephalopathy 17.
- GNAS LOF has many receptor-binding annotations and interacts with DRD1;
  Rotigotine increasing DRD1 is emitted for pseudopseudohypoparathyroidism.
- PTPN11 GOF has insulin-receptor and receptor-tyrosine-kinase binding
  annotations and interacts with EGFR/ERBB2/FLT3; Erlotinib is emitted for
  Noonan syndrome.
- ASXL1 LOF has PPARG-binding activity and interacts with PPARG; Zafirlukast is
  emitted for Bohring-Opitz syndrome.

R431-R434 are rejected. No threshold tuning was attempted after the semantic
failure.

Graph limitation: GO signaling-receptor-binding activities include
intracellular G proteins, phosphatases, chromatin proteins, and other receptor
partners, not only extracellular ligands. SIGNOR physical interaction does not
state ligand-to-receptor activation direction, and increasing or decreasing a
receptor does not necessarily compensate for a variant in an intracellular
binding partner. A viable rule needs an explicit ligand-receptor predicate,
interaction role, and signed signal-flow direction.

## Batch 86: Explicit receptor-ligand activity semantic rescue

Hypothesis: the failed R431-R434 family may have used the wrong GO concept.
Replacing generic signaling-receptor binding (`GO:0005102`) with explicit
receptor-ligand activity (`GO:0048018`) should remove intracellular receptor
partners while retaining extracellular ligands. This is a semantic ablation,
not threshold tuning.

Aggregate coverage query:

```cypher
MATCH (driver:Gene)-[ligand_function:ENABLES]->
      (:MolecularActivity)-[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->
      (:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0048018'})
MATCH (driver)-[interaction:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]-
      (receptor:Gene)
MATCH (receptor)-[receptor_function:ENABLES]->
      (:MolecularActivity)-[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->
      (:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0038023'})
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE driver <> receptor
  AND 'infores:goa' IN
      coalesce(ligand_function.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:signor' IN
      coalesce(interaction.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:goa' IN
      coalesce(receptor_function.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
RETURN count(DISTINCT driver) AS drivers,
       count(DISTINCT receptor) AS receptors,
       count(DISTINCT object) AS diseases,
       count(DISTINCT [driver, receptor, object]) AS paths
```

Coverage:

- 1,672 genes have GOA receptor-ligand activity, represented by 2,315 direct
  `ENABLES` edges before ontology expansion deduplication.
- 58 of those genes have Gene2Phenotype evidence for 70 diseases.
- The complete ligand-SIGNOR-receptor-G2P motif contains 119 distinct paths
  over 38 drivers, 53 receptors, and 48 diseases.

Executable R435:

```cypher
MATCH (driver:Gene)-[ligand_function:ENABLES]->
      (:MolecularActivity)-[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->
      (:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0048018'})
MATCH (driver)-[interaction:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]-
      (receptor:Gene)
MATCH (receptor)-[receptor_function:ENABLES]->
      (:MolecularActivity)-[:SUBCLASS_OF*0..5]->
      (:MolecularActivity {id: 'GO:0038023'})
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE driver <> receptor
  AND 'infores:goa' IN
      coalesce(ligand_function.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:signor' IN
      coalesce(interaction.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:goa' IN
      coalesce(receptor_function.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'gain_of_function_variant_form'
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
WITH DISTINCT driver, receptor, object
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (receptor)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(receptor)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

R436 changes the GOF drug direction to increased. R437 uses LOF/increased, and
R438 uses LOF/decreased.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T201338Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Variant | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R435 | GOF corrective, receptor decreased | 9 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R436 | GOF control, receptor increased | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R437 | LOF corrective, receptor increased | 14 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R438 | LOF control, receptor decreased | 55 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

All outputs are unresolved-or-blind at aggregate level. Partition-safe novel
examples are not credible: R435 predicts Quercetin, Brigatinib, Sunitinib,
Imatinib, Crizotinib, and Ceritinib for Beckwith-Wiedemann syndrome due to an
11p15 imprinting defect. R437 predicts Tretinoin for holoprosencephaly 3 and
solitary median maxillary central incisor syndrome, Ambrisentan for Waardenburg
syndrome 4B, and several kinase inhibitors for developmental syndromes.

Corrective-control ablations:

- R435 versus R436: 6 corrective-only, 3 intersection, 1 control-only pair;
  every cell has zero visible hits.
- R437 versus R438: 6 corrective-only, 8 intersection, and 47 control-only
  pairs; every cell has zero visible hits. The nominal worsening control is
  nearly four times larger than the corrective rule.

Failures:

- The first two comparison invocations used nonexistent `--rule-a` and
  `--rule-b` arguments. The CLI failed fast and was rerun with its documented
  `--parent` and `--child` arguments.
- An exact-path query for three already-classified novel pairs ran for more
  than 90 seconds without returning and was manually interrupted. The query
  joined two GO closures before applying the pair restriction and is an
  inefficient diagnostic shape.

R435-R438 are rejected. The explicit GO class removes the obvious
receptor-binding category error but does not produce treatment enrichment or
signed separation. The remaining graph limitation is unchanged at a deeper
level: SIGNOR physical interaction does not identify ligand-receptor role,
interaction direction, agonism, antagonism, tissue context, or whether a
developmental ligand defect is pharmacologically reversible at the receptor.

## Batch 87: ChEMBL black-box-warning negative evidence

Hypothesis: an indication-independent drug safety property can serve as
negative evidence around an otherwise treatment-free biological rule. R404 was
chosen as the fixed parent because it has an independently corroborated
disease-pathway/target motif and 45 candidates with 13 training hits.

Property inventory:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)
WHERE subject.chembl_black_box_warning IS NOT NULL
RETURN subject.chembl_black_box_warning AS value,
       count(*) AS drugs
ORDER BY drugs DESC
```

The property is populated as the string `True` on 467 drug nodes. Related
categorical properties include 52 withdrawn, 152 discontinued, 1,187
prescription-only, 49 over-the-counter, and 874 natural-product drug nodes.
Only the black-box property was tested in this batch to avoid a broad property
sweep.

Executable R439:

```cypher
MATCH (process:Pathway)-[path_evidence:GENETIC_ASSOCIATION]->
      (object:Disease)
MATCH (target:Gene)-[membership:INVOLVED_IN]->(process)
MATCH (target)-[gene_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
      (target)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
WHERE process.id STARTS WITH 'GO:'
  AND 'infores:geneticskp' IN
      coalesce(path_evidence.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND 'infores:goa' IN coalesce(membership.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND gene_evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND subject.chembl_black_box_warning IS NULL
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

R440 replaces the null check with:

```cypher
AND subject.chembl_black_box_warning = 'True'
```

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T202052Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Property cell | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R404 | parent, any warning status | 45 | 13 | 0 | 2 | 28.89% |
| R439 | no populated black-box warning | 19 | 10 | 0 | 1 | 52.63% |
| R440 | black-box warning | 26 | 3 | 0 | 1 | 11.54% |

The two child cells exactly partition the parent. The unflagged cell retains
76.9% of parent training hits while keeping 42.2% of parent candidates. The
warning cell contains 57.8% of candidates but only 23.1% of training hits.

Retained training examples include Tacrine-Alzheimer disease,
Hydroxychloroquine-EXCESS LMW-DNA, Prednisolone for asthma, Crohn disease,
inflammatory bowel disease, multiple sclerosis, and rheumatoid arthritis, and
Aflibercept or Faricimab for diabetic retinopathy. The gate removes three
valid training indications, all Infliximab: Crohn disease, inflammatory bowel
disease, and rheumatoid arthritis.

Partition-safe novel R439 examples include Quercetin, Curcumin, and
Methylthioninium for Alzheimer disease; Sorafenib or Pegaptanib for diabetic
retinopathy; and Luspatercept for diabetic kidney disease or osteoarthritis.
The retained Prednisolone-heart-failure pair is explicitly contraindicated,
showing that absence of the property is not positive safety evidence. R440 is
dominated by Lenalidomide and Pomalidomide predictions across inflammatory
diseases, plus Tretinoin-Alzheimer disease.

Pareto refresh after catalog-status reconciliation:

- 200 eligible rules among 435 evaluated library entries.
- Global visible surface: 32 rules.
- Global validation surface: 9 rules.
- Treatment-free visible surface: 16 rules.
- Treatment-free validation surface: 10 rules.
- R439 enters the treatment-free visible surface at 52.63% precision and 10
  visible hits from 19 pairs.
- R439 is not on either validation surface because it has zero DRH-only hits.
- R440 is on no surface.

R439 remains a candidate; R440 is rejected. Mechanism score remains medium
because the biological path is inherited from R404 and the property is only a
triage signal.

Graph limitation: black-box warnings are drug-level, jurisdiction- and
label-dependent safety summaries, not indication-specific contraindications.
Missing property values combine genuinely unflagged drugs with incomplete
ChEMBL coverage. The feature can improve precision while still retaining a
known contraindication and removing effective drugs such as Infliximab.

## Batch 88: Protein production and processing correction blocker

Hypothesis: direct drug effects on protein production or processing could
extend the turnover families with distinct corrective mechanisms:

- decreased synthesis for a GOF driver;
- increased synthesis for a LOF driver;
- increased cleavage for a GOF driver;
- decreased cleavage for a LOF driver;
- increased uptake for a LOF driver;
- decreased metabolic processing for a LOF driver.

Opposite directions were included as controls. Aggregate executable probe:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier IN
      ['synthesis', 'cleavage', 'metabolic_processing', 'uptake']
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier IN ['increased', 'decreased']
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier IN
      ['gain_of_function_variant_form', 'loss_of_function_variant_form']
  AND none(
    source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
  )
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN effect.object_aspect_qualifier AS aspect,
       effect.object_direction_qualifier AS direction,
       association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier AS variant,
       count(DISTINCT [subject.id, object.id]) AS pairs,
       count(DISTINCT subject) AS drugs,
       count(DISTINCT driver) AS drivers,
       count(DISTINCT object) AS diseases
ORDER BY aspect, direction, variant
```

Only four cells are nonzero:

| Aspect | Direction | Variant | Pairs |
|---|---|---|---:|
| metabolic processing | decreased | LOF | 1 |
| metabolic processing | increased | LOF | 1 |
| synthesis | increased | GOF | 1 |
| uptake | increased | LOF | 2 |

The synthesis cell is the worsening control; the corrective GOF synthesis
decrease and LOF synthesis increase cells are empty. All cleavage cells are
empty. The same one-pair scale in both LOF metabolic-processing directions
does not support direction discrimination. The two uptake pairs lack a
matched control and the qualifier does not identify the transported species,
origin, destination, or whether uptake restores functional protein.

No executable catalog rules were added. The family is coverage- and
semantics-blocked, not threshold-blocked.

Graph limitation: normalized `AFFECTS` aspects do not preserve protein species,
compartment, cleavage site, synthesis substrate, uptake direction, assay
context, or mutant-versus-wild-type product. The graph also lacks sufficient
variant-qualified overlap for these otherwise plausible mechanisms.

## Batch 89: Explicit inhibitory-antibody correction

Hypothesis: the broad negative-pharmacology union in R389 may conceal a
high-specificity drug-modality mechanism. An explicitly inhibitory antibody
against a GOF disease driver should be more interpretable than generic
inhibition; the same mechanism against a LOF driver is the worsening control.

The first blind-safe aggregate query also profiled antisense inhibition,
inverse agonism, channel blockage, and positive or negative allosteric
modulation:

```cypher
CALL () {
  MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
  MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
  MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
  WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
    AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
    AND effect.causal_mechanism_qualifier IN [
      'antibody_inhibition',
      'antisense_oligonucleotide_inhibition',
      'inverse_agonism',
      'molecular_channel_blockage',
      'negative_allosteric_modulation',
      'positive_allosteric_modulation',
      'partial_agonism'
    ]
    AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
        coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier IN
        ['gain_of_function_variant_form', 'loss_of_function_variant_form']
    AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
    AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
    AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
  RETURN DISTINCT subject, object,
         effect.causal_mechanism_qualifier AS mechanism,
         effect.object_direction_qualifier AS direction,
         association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier AS variant
}
OPTIONAL MATCH
  (subject)-[target:TREATS|AMELIORATES_CONDITION]->(object)
WITH subject, object, mechanism, direction, variant,
     reduce(
       raw = [],
       relation IN collect(target) |
       raw + coalesce(relation.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
     ) AS raw_sources
WITH subject, object, mechanism, direction, variant,
     reduce(
       sources = [],
       source IN raw_sources |
       CASE WHEN source IN sources THEN sources ELSE sources + source END
     ) AS sources
RETURN mechanism, direction, variant,
       count(*) AS pairs,
       sum(
         CASE
           WHEN size(sources) = 1
                AND sources[0] = 'infores:drug-repurposing-hub'
           THEN 1 ELSE 0
         END
       ) AS drh,
       sum(
         CASE
           WHEN size(sources) > 0
                AND NOT (
                  size(sources) = 1
                  AND sources[0] = 'infores:drug-repurposing-hub'
                )
                AND NOT (
                  size(sources) = 1
                  AND sources[0] = 'infores:ttd'
                )
           THEN 1 ELSE 0
         END
       ) AS training
ORDER BY mechanism, variant, direction
```

The original aggregate query also named
`PREVENTATIVE_FOR_CONDITION`, matching the Biolink closure inventory, but
Neo4j emitted an unknown-relationship-type warning because that relationship
is absent from this build. The warning did not change counts.

Aggregate modality cells:

| Mechanism | Variant | Pairs | Training | DRH |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| antibody inhibition | GOF | 6 | 2 | 0 |
| antibody inhibition | LOF control | 11 | 0 | 0 |
| molecular channel blockage | GOF | 292 | 1 | 0 |
| molecular channel blockage | LOF control | 255 | 0 | 0 |
| negative allosteric modulation | GOF | 6 | 0 | 0 |
| negative allosteric modulation | LOF control | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| positive allosteric modulation | GOF control | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| positive allosteric modulation | LOF | 59 | 0 | 0 |

No antisense, inverse-agonist, or partial-agonist cell intersected this
variant-qualified motif. Only antibody inhibition was cataloged.

Executable R441:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->(driver:Gene)
MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(driver)
MATCH (driver)-[association:ASSOCIATED_WITH]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
  AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity'
  AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
  AND effect.causal_mechanism_qualifier IN ['antibody_inhibition']
  AND 'infores:gene2phenotype' IN
      coalesce(association.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
  AND association.subject_form_or_variant_qualifier =
      'gain_of_function_variant_form'
  AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
  AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
  AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
```

R442 substitutes `loss_of_function_variant_form`.

Run artifact: `results/runs/20260812T202631Z/summary.json`.

| Rule | Variant | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| R441 | GOF corrective | 6 | 2 | 0 | 0 | 33.33% |
| R442 | LOF worsening control | 11 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0% |

The two training hits are Amivantamab-lung cancer and
Necitumumab-lung cancer through GOF EGFR. The four partition-safe predictions
are:

- Amivantamab for hereditary papillary renal-cell carcinoma through GOF MET;
- Panitumumab for lung cancer through GOF EGFR;
- Cetuximab for lung cancer through GOF EGFR;
- Ramucirumab for recurrent inflammatory fibroid gastrointestinal polyps
  through GOF PDGFRA.

Exact path provenance:

- Amivantamab antibody-inhibition assertions are from DrugCentral and cite the
  FDA multidisciplinary review; binding to EGFR and MET is independently
  represented by DrugCentral, DGIdb, and ChEMBL.
- Cetuximab, Panitumumab, Necitumumab, and Ramucirumab effect assertions are
  from DGIdb; their bindings are represented by DGIdb and usually ChEMBL.
- Gene2Phenotype GOF disease paths cite publications for EGFR-lung cancer,
  MET-hereditary papillary renal-cell carcinoma, and
  PDGFRA-inflammatory-fibroid polyposis.

The first exact-path command failed before graph execution because the shell
expanded `$pairs` to an empty string. The corrected command constructed the
parameter token inside Python and returned all safe paths.

R441 remains an exploratory high-mechanism candidate. R442 is rejected. R441
is not on any current Pareto surface because other rules achieve the same or
greater recall with higher precision, but it adds a distinct biologic-modality
family rather than a parameter variant.

Graph limitation: antibody inhibition is still not allele-specific and does
not encode tumor expression, somatic versus germline context, antibody access,
or biomarker selection. The six pairs represent only three receptor drivers,
so apparent pair-level precision is not six independent mechanisms.

## Batch 90: Direct signed phenotype correction blocker

Hypothesis: a drug may directly suppress a phenotype manifested by a disease,
providing phenotype-level correction without a gene intermediate. Increased
phenotype direction would be the matched control.

Executable coverage query:

```cypher
MATCH (subject:Drug)-[effect:AFFECTS]->
      (phenotype:PhenotypicFeature)
      <-[:HAS_PHENOTYPE]-(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
RETURN effect.object_aspect_qualifier AS aspect,
       effect.object_direction_qualifier AS direction,
       effect.causal_mechanism_qualifier AS mechanism,
       count(DISTINCT [subject.id, object.id]) AS pairs,
       count(DISTINCT subject) AS drugs,
       count(DISTINCT phenotype) AS phenotypes,
       count(DISTINCT object) AS diseases,
       collect(DISTINCT effect.primary_knowledge_sources)[0..8] AS sources
ORDER BY pairs DESC
LIMIT 40
```

The query returned no rows. The graph has drug-to-phenotype treatment and
side-effect edges, but no qualified causal `AFFECTS` edge that joins directly
to a disease phenotype. No rule was cataloged.

Graph limitation: phenotype-level therapeutic effects are represented as
treatment assertions or adverse events, not signed changes in phenotype
severity, frequency, onset, or duration. A useful symptom-correction rule
needs those signed phenotype outcomes and treatment-independent provenance.

## Batch 91: Transcriptomic signature-reversal blocker

Hypothesis: common-disease treatment prediction could use direct signature
reversal when a disease increases gene expression and a drug decreases the
same genes, or vice versa. Requiring at least two opposed genes would create a
nonlinear disease-state mechanism independent of causal-gene direction.

Disease-to-gene executable inventory:

```cypher
MATCH (object:Disease)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(gene:Gene)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
RETURN effect.object_aspect_qualifier AS aspect,
       effect.object_direction_qualifier AS direction,
       effect.causal_mechanism_qualifier AS mechanism,
       count(*) AS edges,
       count(DISTINCT object) AS diseases,
       count(DISTINCT gene) AS genes,
       collect(DISTINCT effect.primary_knowledge_sources)[0..10] AS sources
ORDER BY edges DESC
LIMIT 50
```

This returned no rows.

The reverse gene-to-disease orientation was also probed:

```cypher
MATCH (gene:Gene)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(object:Disease)
WHERE effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
RETURN effect.object_aspect_qualifier AS object_aspect,
       effect.object_direction_qualifier AS object_direction,
       count(*) AS edges,
       count(DISTINCT object) AS diseases,
       count(DISTINCT gene) AS genes
ORDER BY edges DESC
LIMIT 50
```

It also returned no rows. The first version of this reverse query additionally
requested nonexistent `subject_aspect_qualifier` and
`subject_direction_qualifier` properties. Neo4j emitted unknown-property-key
warnings; removing those fields did not change the empty result.

No rule was cataloged. The family is representation-blocked.

Graph limitation: the build contains abundant drug-to-gene expression effects
but no disease-state expression signature encoded with compatible signed
edges. Gene-disease associations and literature co-occurrence cannot be used
as substitutes for measured up- or down-regulation without changing the
meaning of signature reversal.

## Batch 92: ChEMBL availability-status negative-evidence failure

Hypothesis: withdrawn or discontinued drug status may provide stronger
drug-level negative evidence than an indication-independent black-box warning.
The R404 biological parent was held fixed.

Blind-safe aggregate executable query:

```cypher
CALL () {
  /* Complete executable body of R404. */
  MATCH (process:Pathway)-[path_evidence:GENETIC_ASSOCIATION]->
        (object:Disease)
  MATCH (target:Gene)-[membership:INVOLVED_IN]->(process)
  MATCH (target)-[gene_evidence:MINER_GENE_DISEASE_EVIDENCE]->(object)
  MATCH (subject:Drug)-[binding:DIRECTLY_PHYSICALLY_INTERACTS_WITH]->
        (target)
  MATCH (subject)-[effect:AFFECTS]->(target)
  WHERE process.id STARTS WITH 'GO:'
    AND 'infores:geneticskp' IN
        coalesce(path_evidence.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    AND 'infores:goa' IN coalesce(membership.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
    AND effect.qualified_predicate = 'biolink:causes'
    AND effect.object_aspect_qualifier = 'activity_or_abundance'
    AND effect.object_direction_qualifier = 'decreased'
    AND gene_evidence._miner_disease_rank <= 3
    AND none(
      source IN coalesce(binding.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
      WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
    )
    AND none(
      source IN coalesce(effect.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
      WHERE source IN ['infores:drug-repurposing-hub', 'infores:ttd']
    )
    AND coalesce(object._miner_disease_descendant_degree, 0) <= 500
    AND object._miner_disease_superclass_degree >= 1
    AND object.id STARTS WITH 'MONDO:'
  RETURN DISTINCT subject.id AS subject_id, object.id AS object_id
}
MATCH (subject:Drug {id: subject_id})
MATCH (object:Disease {id: object_id})
OPTIONAL MATCH
  (subject)-[target:TREATS|AMELIORATES_CONDITION]->(object)
WITH subject, object,
     coalesce(subject.chembl_availability_type, 'missing') AS availability,
     reduce(
       raw = [],
       relation IN collect(target) |
       raw + coalesce(relation.primary_knowledge_sources, [])
     ) AS raw_sources
WITH subject, object, availability,
     reduce(
       sources = [],
       source IN raw_sources |
       CASE WHEN source IN sources THEN sources ELSE sources + source END
     ) AS sources
RETURN availability,
       count(*) AS pairs,
       sum(
         CASE
           WHEN size(sources) = 1
                AND sources[0] = 'infores:drug-repurposing-hub'
           THEN 1 ELSE 0
         END
       ) AS drh,
       sum(
         CASE
           WHEN size(sources) > 0
                AND NOT (
                  size(sources) = 1
                  AND sources[0] = 'infores:drug-repurposing-hub'
                )
                AND NOT (
                  size(sources) = 1
                  AND sources[0] = 'infores:ttd'
                )
           THEN 1 ELSE 0
         END
       ) AS training,
       sum(
         CASE
           WHEN EXISTS {
             MATCH (subject)-[:CONTRAINDICATED_IN]->(object)
           }
           THEN 1 ELSE 0
         END
       ) AS contraindicated
ORDER BY pairs DESC
```

Results:

| Availability | Pairs | Training | DRH | Contra | Visible precision |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| prescription only | 30 | 6 | 0 | 1 | 20.00% |
| discontinued | 10 | 7 | 0 | 1 | 70.00% |
| missing | 5 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0% |

No R404 candidate is marked withdrawn. The discontinued cohort is highly
enriched rather than depleted. Its seven training pairs are Tacrine-Alzheimer
disease and six Prednisolone indications: asthma, Crohn disease,
inflammatory bowel disease, multiple sclerosis, rheumatoid arthritis, and
EXCESS LMW-DNA.

No rule was cataloged. Discontinued status reflects market history,
replacement therapies, or risk-benefit decisions and does not imply lack of
efficacy for the disease. The result also demonstrates concentration: seven
hits represent only two drugs.

Graph limitation: ChEMBL availability is not indication-specific, temporally
resolved, or linked to the reason for withdrawal/discontinuation. It cannot
distinguish efficacy failure, safety, commercial decisions, or supersession by
newer therapy.

## Batch 93: Information-content property rejected before tuning

Hypothesis: graph-native node `information_content` might select more
informative drugs, genes, or diseases for common-disease rules without relying
on raw degree.

Executable distribution queries:

```cypher
MATCH (node:Disease)
```
